# Readout — session 1, 2026-09-09

Shape: readout, the one screen (CLAUDE.md §3.2). What was measured, what was assumed,
what was not looked at, and what this session's "done" does not assert.

## Verdict

**Session 1 delivered a plan, STOPPED, and the maintainer ruled.** Seven
deliverables are in commit one; all sixteen rulings are answered
(`DECISIONS.md`); the one approved edit to the constitution is its own
commit. No tool exists. Slice S1 may open.

## What it rests on

| claim | rests on | provenance class |
|---|---|---|
| the census counts (bbh 79 + VS 127 + SMS 94 = 300 count rows) | re-run by a different agent per file: 79/79 MATCH; VS 122 re-run → 121 MATCH + 1 corrected (an "all 0" gloss that failed its own command), and 5 rows added by the producer after verification, re-measured by the producer only; 94/94 MATCH | measured twice by different agents, except the 5 late VS rows (once) |
| the census items (280 + 740 + 228 = 1,248 rows) | 186 + 555 + 107 rows script-generated and re-generated byte-identical by the verifiers; hand-read rows spot-checked 15/15, 14/15, 11/15 — every miss a line-number citation, every one corrected by the producer and recorded | measured; citations corrected after verification |
| the headline numbers (87 / 555 / 66 / 311 / 4,808 / 32 / 121 / F1–F11) | the orchestrator's own run, recorded verbatim in `docs/census/README.md` | measured, three ways |
| the bins (402 keep / 339 generalize / 506 consumer / 1 drop) | one decision per row by a third agent; ids diffed identical to the census; drop-without-reason count printed: 0 | judgement, individually recorded |
| the abstraction, generality proof, fidelity plan | proposals citing `[BBH-N]` rules and census rows; no code | design, UNRATIFIED |
| the slice estimate (14 sessions) | calibrated on bbh's measured pace (10 slices, 19 commits, 3 days, 7 VS sessions) × an **arbitrary** factor 2 for the two new kinds | estimate — not a measurement |
| the corrected citation MJC-52 | grep of the sentence in VampireSaved; anchor `docs/project/gotchas.md:2913-2922` | measured |

## Controls that fired

This session wrote no gate, so no must-fire control exists yet. The protocol
itself was exercised in both directions: verifiers found 1 count-row defect,
1 generator offset defect, 5 wrong line-number citations and 1 regex false
positive across the three census files — all corrected by the producers and
all recorded — and the orchestrator's own "319 gate scripts" was found wrong
(311 gates + 8 library scripts) and corrected in five places. A verification
step that found nothing would have been the suspicious result.

## What this session does NOT assert

- That the abstraction is right. It is a proposal; the first evidence is F12
  diffing empty, and F12 does not exist.
- That the estimate is right. The factor 2 is arbitrary and says so.
- That any lineage number is *current* beyond the recorded HEADs
  (`f675710`, `5df1d8be`, `ecc5481`). A moved HEAD invalidates the census
  until the recount is re-run (R9).
- That bbh's own rules are correct — the census counts and locates them; it
  does not test them.
- Anything about F8 / F21 (MAME, ROMs): not run, not looked at.
- Anything about the content of VampireSaved's `STATE_HISTORY.md` (28,918
  lines), its 370 untracked build logs, its emulator submodules, or SMS's
  Lua probe bodies and traces — listed per file in each census's §D.
- That the two installed skills `romhacking-methodology` and `snes-romhacking`
  (plain copies, Aug 12) match any repository — not diffed; SMS and
  VampireSaved cite `[RH-N]` from the former (VS: 72 citations in 43 files).

## What was assumed

- The three local clones are the repositories the prompt names (checked by
  remote URL, not by directory name — G6).
- The maintainer's three rulings R6–R8 as answered in plan mode.
- That reading a rule's first sentence (≤ 140 chars) is enough to bin it —
  found false once (MJC-47) and handled by reading the source; not re-checked
  for the other 1,247 rows.

## Counts, separately (BBX-1)

|---|---|
| gates run | 0 (none exist) |
| PASS / SKIP / FAIL / TIMEOUT | 0 / 0 / 0 / 0 |
| controls declared / fired | 0 / 0 |
| fidelity rows run | 0 of F12–F21 planned |

| NOT-RECOUNTABLE rows | 54 (VS 27, SMS 27; ids printed by the gate) — the census's uncovered claims, allowed to move only downward |
| census rows rewritten to reach this (document first, no count changed) | bbh 65 by script + 9 by hand; VS 22; SMS 34 |

## What it rests on

The recorded HEADs (`f675710`, `5df1d8be`, `ecc5481`), checked before any row runs; the hermetic environment (D6); the census grammar (`docs/census/README.md`); every count's own command.

## What this green does NOT assert

- Anything about §B item rows or §C generators: the recount re-runs §A counts only. The 1,248 item rows and the 848 generated rows were verified once, by the verifiers, through the interactive shell (G9 applies to any of them that used a recursive grep — none of the generators did; the §B spot-checks read files by line, not by grep).

- That the counts are *current*: they are the counts at the recorded HEADs, and a moved HEAD turns the whole file red until it is re-measured.
- That the commands measure what their dimension says. The recount proves the command prints the number; whether the command is the right question is the reviewer's (G10 is the example).
- Portability: measured on macOS only so far (R3 wants Linux and WSL; the platform gates are later in S1).

# bbx-2 step 1 — R18: the recount on a clone, the tests provably read-only (2026-09-09)

## Verdict

`BBX_BBH_HOME=~/Developer/blackbox-harness bin/bbx selftest`, alone → **GREEN**, 156 s:

```
PASS 9     SKIP 0     FAIL 0     MISSING 0
controls fired 14 / declared 14; gates with no declaration: 0; red: 0
ok: no tracked file changed during the run
NOTE  fidelity_bbh  bbh-untracked before=0 after=0
```

`gates/census_recount.sh` alone: `PASS: 3 census files, 300 rows: 244 match, 0 mismatch, 0 refused, 56 not recountable, 1 matched with a non-zero exit; 4 controls fired`, 32 s.

## What changed

The maintainer ruled R18: the census and the tests work on a clone or make explicit, and prove, that the tree is not changed. `lib/py/bbx/recount.py` now exports the recorded commit as a shared clone under `TMPDIR`, runs every row there, removes the clone, refuses a command that names the repository's absolute path, and reports a moved lineage tip as one `NOTE: drift …` line after re-running the rows on a clone of the tip. `gates/fidelity_bbh.sh` declares READ-ONLY on bbh's tree and proves it (tracked porcelain before = after, or FAIL). Census grammar rule 7; defaults D17–D19; D2 re-measured.

## Counts, separately

| | |
|---|---|
| controls declared / fired, `census_recount` | 4 / 4 (wrong-head → "HEAD unknown"; moved-count; names-the-tree → REFUSED; drift-detected → `ahead=1 rows_moved=1 ids=A2` on a shadow recorded at bbh's parent `b4852e5`) |
| drift on real data | the bbx-1 VampireSaved census (recorded `5df1d8be`) against the tip: `ahead=2 rows_moved=1 ids=A77` — the same one row the hand re-measure found; 42.8 s |
| shared clone of VampireSaved | 1.76 s, 363 MB (its `.git` is 949 MB; nothing written under it; `git worktree list` there unchanged) |
| census rows the clone exposed as host facts | 4: bbh A3 (4 → 0), SMS A4 (0 = 0, a row that could never fail), SMS A13 (5332 → 30 tracked), SMS A14 (324 → 37 tracked) — G13 |
| not-recountable rows, before → after | 54 → 56 (bbh 0 → 1, SMS 27 → 28): the two `host:` cells; the only upward move allowed, with G13 as the reason |
| recountable rows now measuring the commit, not the tree | 244 / 244 (every `ls` row is now a fact of the checkout, not of whatever the host had untracked) |
| bbh tree during fidelity | tracked porcelain 4 = 4; untracked 0 = 0 |
| lineage tips and porcelain at this run | bbh `f675710` / 4; VampireSaved `0cdd9726` / 377 (live: another session's work, not BBX's); SMS `ecc5481` / 0 |
| runtimes | census gate 21 → 32 s (D2); fidelity 37 s measured (D19; header had said ~10 s); selftest 117 → 156 s (D14), the sweep-runner gate 55 → 66 s under a host running VampireSaved's sweep at the same time |
| rulings | R18 in force; R19 (pull queue, N workers over N clones), R20 (fidelity on a clone of bbh) open |

## What it rests on

The four controls of the census gate, each failing (or, for drift, firing) for its stated reason on a shadow of bbh's census; the fidelity gate's before/after snapshot; the clone mechanism measured on the largest lineage; and the recount's summary line carrying `tree=clone tip= ahead= porcelain=` so the readout can tell a rotted census from a moved lineage.

## What this green does NOT assert

- That the fidelity gate is read-only on bbh's *untracked* files beyond the count (0 = 0 here; a gate that wrote and deleted a file inside the run would not show). Moving fidelity to a clone (R20) would make this structural.
- That `--in-place` is read-only: it is declared so and unproved; it exists for a verifier's spot-check on a tree that is already a clone.
- That the census's 56 not-recountable rows are true at their commits.
- Anything about the pull queue (R19): the sweep runner still batches with a barrier.
- Portability of the clone step beyond macOS (R3): `git clone --shared` is used on Linux the same way, not yet measured there.

# bbx-2 step 3 — R19: the pull queue and clone-per-slot in the sweep runner (2026-09-09)

## Verdict

`BBX_BBH_HOME=~/Developer/blackbox-harness bin/bbx selftest`, alone → **GREEN**, 223 s (loaded host):

```
PASS 10    SKIP 0     FAIL 0     MISSING 0
controls fired 20 / declared 20; gates with no declaration: 0; red: 0
ok: no tracked file changed during the run
```

## What changed

`bin/bbx-run-sweep --jobs N` no longer batches: a FIFO holds one token per slot, a worker blocks reading a token, runs its gate, writes the token back (VampireSaved 14z-144, lifted; the loop stays in the main shell). `[sweep].clone_per_slot = true` (D21, off by default) gives every slot, the serial prereq lane included, a plain clone of the consumer's HEAD under `TMPDIR`, removed at exit; the banner names the commit and the working tree's porcelain. `gates/sweep_runner.sh` sections 15–16 measure both.

## Counts, separately

| | |
|---|---|
| queue, measured by the gates' own stamps | `--jobs 2`: the third 1-s gate started 2 s BEFORE the 4-s gate ended (a barrier or a line cannot); `--jobs 1`: 3 s AFTER (control `serial-order`) |
| clone-per-slot | 2 workers, 2 trees, neither the base, both at the fixture's HEAD; base tree untouched by two file-writing gates; clone dir removed; without clones the same gates left 2 files in the base (control `no-clone-dirties`) |
| controls declared / fired, `sweep_runner` | 4 / 4 (env-default-export, prereq-stop, serial-order, no-clone-dirties) |
| fidelity F14 after the runner change | every pair identical (--list 8 lines, --list all 9, --dry-run 32, real run 35, 8 fingerprint pairs) |
| runtimes | sweep-runner gate 90 s alone (sections 15–16 add ~15 s of deliberate sleeps); selftest 223 s (D14) |
| defaults | D21 added (`clone_per_slot`, arbitrary: off because a clone measures HEAD, not the working tree) |

## What it rests on

Stamps printed by the stub gates (`date +%s`), never the runner's own accounting; `pwd -P` and `git rev-parse` from inside the workers; the base tree's listing after the run; F14 against bbh's own runner at `--jobs 1`.

## What this green does NOT assert

- Speed-up on a real consumer: the 1.65× → 3.48× figures are VampireSaved's, quoted from its source, not re-measured here (no instrument-tier consumer exists in BBX yet).
- Clone-per-slot on a consumer with uncommitted work: the clone is HEAD; the banner says so, nothing refuses.
- A gate that escapes its clone by absolute path: not guarded (the recount's path guard is the census's, not the sweep's).
- That the queue's row order in `results.tsv` is stable: it is not, by design; `--resume` keys by name.
- Portability beyond macOS (R3): `mkfifo` and `exec 8<>` are POSIX, not yet run on Linux or WSL.

  classify: that a verdict word printed by a gate outside the runner is read at all: the classifier reads exit status first, then the log; a PASS printed after a non-zero exit is FAIL by design
  config: the meaning of a consumer's keys: only that the layers resolve (consumer over kind profile over kind-blind default) and that dumps are stable
  tier: that a gate reaching an instrument through a path the source regex does not match is seen: the depth and the regex are the limit
  static_runner: the sweep runner or any gate that needs an instrument: this is the pre-commit chain only
  static_runner: runtimes as anything but this host under this load
  controls: that a control is RIGHT — only that a declared control fired and an undeclared one is red (docs/controls.md)
  sweep_runner: speed-up on a real consumer: the queue is measured on stub gates with sleeps, not on an instrument-tier suite
  sweep_runner: a gate that escapes its clone by an absolute path: clone-per-slot pins the cwd, nothing more
  sweep_runner: portability beyond macOS: mkfifo and exec 8<> are POSIX, not yet run on Linux or WSL
  fingerprint: the identity of any artifact that is not a single file: the kind-blind fingerprint is file-sha1 (D16)
  rulings_shape: the prose of a ruling: only its heading, its answer line and its DECISIONS row are read
  readout: that a declared blind spot is true or complete: the screen prints what the header says
  readout: the sweep runner's runs: only bbx-run-static --log is read
  census_recount: the truth of the not-recountable rows at their commits: they are named and counted, never run
  census_recount: that a hand-read citation says what its row claims: only that the line exists
  fidelity_bbh: anything about a suite, a comparator or an expectation: slice S2
  fidelity_bbh: bbh's example's correctness (G11 is bbh's to fix)
  fidelity_bbh: F15 unless BBX_FIDELITY_F15=1 was set for the run
  gates declaring no blind spot: 0   (a gate nobody has asked what its green leaves out)
```

## What changed

`bin/bbx-run-static --log DIR` keeps a run (results.tsv, every gate's log, controls.txt, run.txt with the subject's HEAD, porcelain, platform and the runner's own verdict) and prints nothing extra (F13 identical). `lib/py/bbx/readout.py` reads a kept run into the screen above: RO1's content line by line, RO2 from the gates' new `# NOT-ASSERTED:` header lines (all eleven gates declare; the screen counts any that do not), RO3 by construction. `gates/readout.sh` holds it to a fixture with three controls. The census gate prints its coverage as a NOTE the screen reads. R21 raised (no Linux/WSL host on this machine).

## What changed (step 4, on R24)

`lib/py/bbx/provenance.py`: the register is `PROVENANCE.toml` inside the expectation tree — one bare table per row, the file a VALUE, every field named — complete both ways over every regular file under the tree (the excluded basenames and the register itself aside), every `class` EQUAL to one of R11's eight, no file named twice, a missing field refused by name, a duplicate table refused by the subset parser; `testimony` and `fixture` rows counted and NAMED as not evidence; `--histogram <set>`. `gates/provenance.sh` holds it with four controls. `lib/py/bbx/readout.py`: a kept SUITE run gets its own screen — the findings counted apart (BBX-4), the expectations relied upon as the register's histogram for the set, the comparator classes that PASSed on a pairing whose row is not `fixture` or `testimony`, BBX-14 against a second run; and the static screen's tree line shows the kept run's untracked entry count (G17's mechanism, `bin/bbx-run-static --log` recording it, printing nothing). `gates/readout.sh` gained three controls. D31, D32. bbh moved to `10a82d2` at the open (G11 fixed there; the four dirty files committed): both fidelity gates PASS against it by the D20 override; R28 raised to re-baseline.

  BBX-14 (more than one run): each scenario ran 2 times inside this run (nondeterminism is a finding above); pass --against a second kept run for run-to-run
what this green does NOT assert:

# S2 — the slice readout (CLAUDE.md §7; `docs/slices.md`'s row for S2)

1. **Gates green, controls fired on purpose.** The battery: 19 gates, 55 / 55 controls declared / fired (S2's 25: temporal 4, thresholds 3, compare_dispatch 3, expectation_kinds 3, suite 5, fidelity_bbh_s2 3, provenance 4; readout's 4 new of 7), twice at one HEAD, BBX-14 met (the screen above). Every control's firing is in its gate's log; every gate declares its blind spots.
2. **Fidelity exact.** F12 (17 pairs), F16 (35), F17 (52): the suite runner, the dispatcher with its guards and enumeration, the five temporal checkers and the proposer, over bbh's example on the fake driver and the lineage's synthetic shapes — every diff empty on a plain clone of `f675710` (`gates/fidelity_bbh_s2.sh`), and, measured once at the open, on a clone of `10a82d2`. Three verdict-text controls (one changed string each) make the diffs non-empty. The series continued F12, F16, F17; F13–F15 unchanged.
3. **Every frozen expectation's provenance class; every default in the register.** BBX freezes no expectation of its own in S2 (its `self` kind has no scenarios), so there is nothing to class — and the register tool, its gate and the screen are what would say so for a consumer that does. Defaults D23–D32, each with its class: reference-calibrated (D23 the thresholds, D26 and D27's frame-driven values), principled (D24, D25, D28–D32, the kind-blind values).
4. **What the green does not assert.** On the screen, per gate; and for the slice: no expectation's provenance is stated anywhere yet (no consumer of BBX has a register; bbh's example has bbh's page, F19 in S6); every PASS of every class is on a fixture-class pairing; one driver (bbh's fake), one consumer (bbh's example); the mapped-state comparator not lifted (BBX-25); Darwin arm64 only (R21); a ruling id in `[thresholds].rulings` is any non-empty string.
5. **Rules re-anchored or still inherited.** Still `[inherited]` in CLAUDE.md (S5 promotes); re-anchored in fact this slice: BBX-4 (the finding column, G18's neighbour), BBX-13 (R25's refusal), BBX-24 and BBX-26 (G17), BBX-1 (G18), BBX-17 (a duplicate row refused), BBX-3 (testimony never green), BBX-25 (`compare_fields.py` left in bbh).
6. **STATE, HANDOFF, gotchas, rulings, registries.** Rewritten at this close; G17, G18 filed with prices; R23–R27 answered, R28 raised; the registries complete both ways (the runner's anti-orphan line in every battery).

**What this slice measured that the plan predicted differently:** the fidelity rows live in one gate, not per row; `compare_fields.py` stays in bbh; the register is TOML, not TSV (R24); step 4 cost part of one sitting, not one (the plan's estimate for S2 was 2 sessions: it took bbx-3 and the first half of bbx-4).

## What changed

**The re-baseline (R28, reclassified by the maintainer: a procedure under R8/R20, not a ruling).** Measured first by the D20 override: F12, F13, F14, F14f, F16, F17 identical on a clone of `10a82d2`. Then moved in one commit: both fidelity gates' default (`BBX_BBH_BASELINE:-10a82d2`); D20; D12's five `[sweep].placeholders` in the frame-driven profile (by the row's definition: bbh's defaults verbatim at the baseline); the dated line in `docs/rebaselines.md` (printed at the head of every fidelity run); the census `docs/census/bbh.md` re-measured (A2 19 → 20, every other row and citation unchanged; the recount at the new HEAD: 79 rows, 78 match, 1 not recountable); HANDOFF's dirty-tree hazard retired (bbh is clean); G11 closed as fixed in bbh. bbh moved once more during the sitting (`447e5d2`: its README names the renamed remote, BBH-frame-based): a NOTE on the screen, nothing BBX measures.

**Two findings raised from bbh's work on its own must-fire contract, the maintainer's.** R30, ruled and built: the gate header is the LEADING COMMENT BLOCK — every `#` line after the shebang up to the first non-comment line; a bare `#` no longer ends it (bbh measured 264 of its 315 gates with a bare separator within five lines, colliding with its gate index). BBX had two readers of that block; now one (`controls.py` `header_lines`, imported by `readout.py`); `gates/controls.sh` proves a declaration after a bare `#` is read and one after the first code line is not (the control `body-is-not-header`); over the 19 gates the old and the new reader agree: 55 declarations. R29, raised and open: executable controls — the runner invokes a gate with `CONTROL=<name>`, the gate perturbs its REAL input and runs to its verdict, and the runner requires the gate's own FAIL, because a self-reported FIRED can be printed by a control that tests nothing (bbh's 14z-144 case). Recommended for slice S6 with the cadence left to the tier.

| | |
|---|---|
| gates / controls | 19 / 56 (controls.sh gained `body-is-not-header`) |
| fidelity on the new baseline | F13a–e, F14, F14f (`fidelity_bbh`), F12 17 + F16 35 + F17 52 (`fidelity_bbh_s2`): every diff empty on the plain clone at `10a82d2` |
| the census at `10a82d2` | bbh.md 79 rows: 78 match, 0 mismatch, 1 not recountable; A2 moved 19 → 20 |
| kept battery runs this sitting | 4: the open GREEN 19/55 (bbh at `10a82d2`, a NOTE); after the re-baseline GREEN 19/55; the two close runs above |
| rulings | R28 answered and reclassified; R30 answered and built; R29 open; R21 open |
| bbh's tip at the close | `447e5d2`, 1 past the baseline (README only); porcelain 0; remote renamed BBH-frame-based (the old URL redirects) |

## What it rests on

The two fidelity gates on a plain clone of the new baseline, clean after; the recount on a clone of the new census HEAD; the controls gate's two new cases and its control; every other gate unchanged and green twice.

## What this green does NOT assert

- Everything the screen lists; plus: R29 is a contract on the page's future, not a mechanism — no control here is yet driven by the runner on the gate's real path; every FIRED is still the gate's self-report.
- bbh at `447e5d2` is measured by nothing but a NOTE; the procedure follows it when a sitting needs to (R28).
- Platform: Darwin arm64 only (R21).

## What changed

**The open.** GREEN on `415c88b` (19 gates, 56 / 56 controls, tree unchanged): the bbx-5 close re-derived, the two expected drift NOTEs present (VampireSaved 14 past the census, bbh one past the baseline). Read off that screen: the static run's sentence `expectations relied upon: none registered — the expectation register with its provenance classes is slice S2; until then no comparison against a frozen expectation is claimed` — true at bbx-2, false since bbx-4, printed on the bbx-4 and bbx-5 close screens in this file, unnoticed, because `gates/readout.sh` held only the prefix `none registered`. Fixed: the generated sentence now says what is true by construction (a static run compares against no frozen expectation; a kept suite run carries its register's histogram, D32) and the gate holds the full clause; the module docstring's twin sentence fixed with it. G19, paid 0, the first re-anchor of BBX-10 (rot class 4, a stale reference — inside a GENERATED artifact, the one place a reader trusts most). Its learning (R27) is a mechanism, filed for S6: a rot gate over generated and printed text that refuses "until slice N" sentences outside a history ledger.

**S3 planned, STOPPED.** `docs/plans/S3.md`, the first plan for a kind with no ancestor to diff against. Measured on plain clones at the census commits (never a working tree): SMS `tools/checkdocs.py` at `ecc5481` — 1,500 lines, 37 hand checks, 17 table validators each re-run at a wrong base, 5 sentence forms bound by `docaddrs.py` (557 lines), 55 `says(` quotations, 4 control families; census rows A35–A41 re-derived by `bin/bbx recount docs/census/sms.md --only A35,…,A41` on the clone, 7 of 7 match. VampireSaved `tools/checkdocs_rom.py` at `0cdd9726` — 815 lines, 15 checks, 14 PARAPHRASE mentions, its gate 205 lines with 5 must-fire controls, its covered set 31 rows compared as a multiset (measured once by the commands in the plan; not census rows). bbh's `example/` as the fixture consumer's model (fakesys 314 lines, 6 replays, 4 registry rows). BBX's own plug-in points at `415c88b`: the suite's `*.rpl` literal and one-kind-per-scenario precedence, `compare.sh`'s single family branch, `logfmt.frames()` reading an integer point, `dir_sha1` non-recursive, D28's driver home "absent until S3". The design, contract by contract (§3); the fixture (§4: designed counts, measured when built); fourteen controls in five gates (§5); the readout's coverage line from a suite run (§6); the defaults to register (§7); five steps (§8); what the plan does not decide (§9). Four rulings raised, R31–R34, each with its measured context, recommendation, declined alternatives and cost of being wrong.

**Caught by the document gates on the way, as designed:** `rulings_shape` — DECISIONS' open line still listed R21 alone (updated to R21, R31–R34); `close_sweeps` — G19's first wording named the three deferral tokens in prose (reworded). Both gates green to a file with their exits tested (G16's lesson).

# bbx-7 step 1 — the document-set profile, the scenario extension, the fixture and its generator (2026-09-10)

**The open.** GREEN on `d9b363b` (19 gates, 56 / 56 controls, tree unchanged, the two drift NOTEs): the bbx-6 close re-derived. R34's TOML revision validated by the maintainer at the open.

## What changed

The `document-set` kind profile in `lib/py/bbx/config.py` (D33): portable, no instrument, the scenario a claim set, the identity file-sha1 over the artifact with the whole-set key over its directory (documents and artifact together), seven expectation kinds (the kind-blind three plus `truth` exact, `claims` set, `covered` set, `schema` schema), no temporal family. `[suite].scenario_ext` (D34, R32): kind-blind `rpl`, document-set `claims`, read by `bin/bbx-run-suite`'s loop and by `lib/sh/expectation_kinds.sh` through a new `bbx.expectations scenario-ext`. The fixture generator `fixture/docset/mkdocset.py` (`--check` regenerates under TMPDIR and diffs; ten chirality predicates named, asserted on the design and on the tree's artifact; D35, D36, D37) and what it writes — BBX's second consumer, `fixture/docset/` with its own `bbx.toml`. `gates/docset_fixture.sh`, portable tier.

**Built in a shadow tree first**, as the handoff prescribes; the shadow caught one defect before the tree saw it: the enumeration helper reused `_ee_ext` for each file's own extension and clobbered the scenario extension read a line earlier, so every enumeration matched nothing and returned 0 — the kinds gate's three controls read DEAD and the fixture gate's `unknown-kind-under-profile` refused to fire. One rename; no mechanism (R27: the practice is the learning, as at bbx-4). Then in the shadow: `fidelity_bbh_s2` PASS (F12, F16, F17: 104 pairings identical after the loop change), `suite` PASS (5 controls).

# bbx-8 step 2 — the document-set extractor, the driver, the truth kind and the driver gate (2026-09-10)

**The open.** GREEN on `e398abd` (20 gates, 59 / 59 controls, tree unchanged; the two drift NOTEs: bbh one ahead, VampireSaved 19 ahead): the bbx-7 close re-derived. No ruling was pending but R21.

## What changed

`lib/py/bbx/docset.py`: the extractor. It QUOTES a claim from a document (the line, verbatim — the `text` view), DERIVES the fact the line names from the artifact (`<name>.<field>=<value>` — the `artifact` view, keyed by the first column, D39), BINDS the two into one of the five closed statuses (D37: BOUND, MISMATCH, PARAPHRASE against the listed literal, UNBINDABLE with its listed reason, STALE when the record or column is gone or a listed line no longer exists), and writes the point-indexed log the harness already reads: `<index> <status>:<sha1-quoted>:<sha1-derived>` then `END <n>` (R31; `logfmt.py` untouched, F17 still green). The two strings and the token are defined there and nowhere else (D38); `split_token` is the one splitter. A line that matches no declared form and is not listed, but states an absence or carries a digit, is REFUSED naming `<document>:<line>` (D40, BBX-7). Every run begins with a self-test on synthetic lines against a synthetic record: the true line BOUND, the mirrored value MISMATCH (BBX-15), the loose line not found — a form that stopped matching is named before any document is read. `bbx.docset map` prints the index → (document, line, form, status, quoted, derived) table a triage needs; `bbx.docset summary` prints the coverage NOTEs the suite will carry (R33).

`drivers/docset.sh` (bbh's four arguments; `DOCSET_PATH` a `;` search path made absolute component by component; the out file removed before the run; exit 0 only with an `END` line; 3 REFUSED on a form no extractor implements, a view, `DOCSET_VIEW`, `DOCSET_FORMS`, and the whole frame-driven and guard families; 1 DISCARDED on an unreadable artifact, document or claim set, or a failed self-test; `DOCSET_NONDET=1` salts every token with the clock). `drivers/README.md`: the contract cited from bbh, the exit vocabulary, one driver row. `fixture/docset/mkdocset.py` now writes the `truth` kind — `expected/fixture/<s>.truth` (`exact fixture`) and `expected/fixture/logs/<s>.log` — from the DESIGN's statuses with `docset.py`'s strings, never by running the driver; `--check` covers them (the plan's separate `--truth` flag was not built: a truth every generation writes is a truth `--check` verifies). The register gains six rows, all `fixture` (16 rows: 12 expectations, 3 truth logs, the registry). `gates/docset_driver.sh`, portable, 7 controls; `gates/portable.txt` 17 rows.

**Built in a shadow tree first.** The shadow caught one defect before the tree saw it (G20): the `extractor-shadow` control's first run read DEAD because the self-test's own true line, with its regex broken, tripped the number guard and the driver printed a REFUSED line about a synthetic sentence instead of naming the form. The self-test now catches the refusal on its true line. And the gate's first run corrected the plan (G21): deleting the artifact's first row makes the two claims about that record STALE, not every bound claim MISMATCH — a keyed view is not a positional one. The plan was corrected in its own commit (`d230fc7`, BBX-19; retraction X9) before the gate asserted the measured shape.

## What it rests on

The seven gates run in the tree to a file with their exits tested (`docset_driver` 7 controls, `docset_fixture` 3, `provenance`, `expectation_kinds` 3, `controls`, `readout`, `close_sweeps` 3); the generator's own `--check` on the tree; the two close batteries below.

## What this green does NOT assert

- The verdict text of the `exact`, `set` and `schema` comparators (step 3): the gate compares the log to the truth with `cmp` and `diff` and names the index itself.
- The suite over the fixture — the identity refusal, the loop over the kinds, `--freeze`, the coverage NOTE on the screen (step 4). Until then `NOTE: coverage 20/23` is printed by a gate, not by a suite run.
- Prose, reasoning and causal claims in a document: only sentences in a declared form and the listed rows are claims. The truth of the artifact itself: a document that agrees with a wrong artifact reads BOUND.
- That D39's lexical classes and D40's guards are anybody's but the fixture's (arbitrary: the first real consumer is the detector).
- A nested document tree or a second artifact per set (`DOCSET_VIEW` is refused).
- Executable controls (R29, S6): every `CONTROL FIRED` here is the gate's self-report.

**Retractions this sitting:** X9 (the plan's shifted-artifact prediction; the plan corrected in its own commit `d230fc7`, BBX-19, before the gate asserted the measured shape). **Incidents reviewed for learnings (R27, step 10):** two — G20 (the self-test's blind spot, found by the `extractor-shadow` control in the shadow tree, paid 1 gate run: a trap, the control is the mechanism) and G21 (a positional prediction from a keyed view, paid 0: a mechanism candidate for S4's positional view, nothing built). One hazard filed in HANDOFF for step 4: `--freeze` and the `.truth` kind name the same `logs/<s>.log`.

**What this close does NOT assert:** the `exact` / `set` / `schema` comparators' verdict text and the suite over the fixture (steps 3–4: the truth is compared by `cmp` inside a gate); a real document set (every expectation is `fixture` class, the forms and guards are the fixture's); bbh's tip beyond the baseline; any platform but Darwin arm64 (R21); executable controls (R29, S6: every FIRED is a self-report); and everything S2's slice readout listed.

# bbx-9 step 3 — the three comparator families of the document-set kind, and the truth logs that were never in the repository (2026-09-10)

**The open.** GREEN on `6a80ec7` (`build/selftest_20260910_140052`: 21 gates, 66 / 66 controls, tree unchanged; the two drift NOTEs: bbh one ahead, VampireSaved 21 ahead): the bbx-8 close re-derived. No ruling was pending but R21.

## What changed

**The first finding was not step 3's.** The shadow tree, built with `git archive HEAD` as the handoff prescribes, had no `fixture/docset/expected/fixture/logs/`: the three truth logs the bbx-8 close counted ("register 16 rows") sat under the `.gitignore` rule `*.log` and were never staged. In the shadow `gates/docset_fixture.sh` went red (`--check`: 3 files DIFFER) and `gates/docset_driver.sh` went red on every truth comparison — the first time either ran on what the repository actually holds. A clone, R21's Linux run included, would have opened red. Gotcha G22, paid one shadow run. Fixed: `!fixture/**/*.log`, the three logs added; and the mechanism (R27): `bbx.provenance` now requires every file a register row names to be one git TRACKS when the expectation tree is inside a work tree, reports the check as *not run* outside one (never as passed), and `gates/provenance.sh` proves it with the control `row-untracked` (a registered, present, ignored file FAILs naming it); `gates/docset_fixture.sh` now runs the tool over the fixture's REAL register (16 rows, complete, every file tracked) instead of leaving it to `--check`'s diff alone.

**Step 3, as planned (§8.3), built in the shadow and moved into the tree once green there.** Three comparators under the one dispatcher (R23), each printing ONE verdict line in BBX's own text — no ancestor prints these, so the family's gate is the freeze (C4):

- `lib/py/bbx/compare_exact.py` — the truth log against the run log BY INDEX: `PASS exact (<n> indices, every token the truth's)`; `FAIL exact: index <i> differs (truth <status>, run <status>; quoted same|differs, derived same|differs)` when both tokens split as document-set tokens (the one splitter), so a triage reads which half moved; `FAIL-SHORT exact: the run ends at index <m>, the truth at <n> (…)` — `short`, apart from `diverged` (BBX-4, C3); a gap, an END that disagrees with its indices, an index the truth lacks and a duplicated index each FAIL naming the index; an empty truth is `nothing compared` (bbh's #54 lesson); a missing truth log is bbh's `NO-BASE-LOG`.
- `lib/py/bbx/compare_set.py` — a frozen MULTISET of `(document, line, form, status)` rows (named TOML fields, R34's caveat) against the run's rows, which are the log's statuses joined by index with the claim map — `bbx.docset run_rows`, which PROVES the map describes this log through the quoted-hash half of every token (D38) and refuses otherwise. Two consumers inside the family (BBX-25): `claims` (mode `inventory`, both ways: `FAIL set-inventory: <a> frozen row(s) not in the run (first …); <b> run row(s) not frozen (first …)`) and `covered` (mode `shrink-only`, R33: a lost row `FAIL set-covered: no longer covers <row> (<k> lost)`; growth `PASS set-covered (<n> frozen rows still covered; the run covers <k> more)` plus `NOTE: covered-grew <k>`; a frozen row whose status is not BOUND or PARAPHRASE refused). A duplicate row on either side FAILs naming it — the binder never produces one, so a duplicate is a hand edit (BBX-17).
- `lib/py/bbx/compare_schema.py` — the artifact's SHAPE before any value (R34, BBX-16): header names in order, one type per column from `int | hex | str | nonempty` (D41), the rows line (`>=` or `=`); the FIRST violation named — `column <i> is '<got>' (frozen '<want>')`, `row <r> has <m> columns (frozen <n>)` (the maintainer's fifteenth control), `row <r> column '<name>' = '<value>' is not <type>`, `rows <m> (frozen <op> <n>)`. A blank line inside the artifact is a row with one empty column and fails on shape; nothing is skipped silently.
- `lib/sh/compare.sh` — `compare_check <expdir> <name> <kind> <spec> <runmask> <log> [<scenario> <artifact>]`: the temporal branch is untouched (F16 green); `exact)` reads the expectation file's `[spec].baseset` and pairs `<root>/<baseset>/logs/<name>.log` (the temporal family's own pairing); `set)` needs the two trailing arguments; `schema)` the artifact. A kind outside the profile in force is still `FAIL kind '<k>' has no comparator family …` — a `.masked` under `document-set` and a `.truth` under `frame-driven` both measured.
- `lib/py/bbx/docset.py` — two verbs: `rows` (the join above) and `resolve <set>`, the ONE resolver over `DOCSET_PATH` (`;`-separated, each component made absolute, an absent absolute component skipped, a relative one that resolves nowhere an error); `drivers/docset.sh` now calls it instead of carrying its own loop, so the schema comparator and the driver read the same artifact by construction. `bin/bbx compare exact|set|schema` reaches the tools.
- `lib/py/bbx/finding.py` — one rule generalised: `FAIL unknown ` (bbh has exactly one such line, `masked_compare.sh:127` at `10a82d2`, measured by `git grep`) so `FAIL unknown exact class`, `set mode`, `schema type`, `schema rows op` all read `unknown-class`; no new word (§8.3's prediction held: `diverged`, `short`, `no-base-log`, `unknown-class`, `pass` cover every line).
- `gates/set_schema.sh` (portable, 5 controls, ~7 s): every kind of every scenario PASSes through the dispatcher (12 pairings); the refusals; the five controls below; and every verdict line the gate saw is fed to `finding.py` — a line reading `unclassified` fails the gate (`NOTE: verdict-lines-frozen 38`).
- `docs/defaults.md` D41 (the schema type vocabulary, principled/ruled), D42 (the kind's NOTE keys). The plan's §5 and §8.3 had placed the third profile's unknown-kind control in `gates/expectation_kinds.sh`; it was built in `gates/docset_fixture.sh` at bbx-7 — the plan corrected in its own commit `08c9fef` (BBX-19; retraction X10) and nothing was added to `expectation_kinds.sh`.

**The tree's classifier caught what the shadow had not (G23, paid two close batteries):** the gate's section-5 banner quoted `unclassified` in backticks inside double quotes; the shell ran the word, printed `command not found`, and the gate went on to PASS with exit 0. The shadow had run it bare and tested the exit — green. `bin/bbx selftest` read the log through the classifier's shell-error clause (lineage G18) and both first close batteries were NOT GREEN on that one gate (`PASS 21 FAIL 1`, all 72 controls fired: `build/selftest_20260910T121903Z`, `…T122414Z`). The quote fixed; the shadow practice now runs every touched gate through `bin/bbx classify` (HANDOFF). BBX-1 re-anchored a second time.

**The shadow caught two things before the tree saw either:** G22 above, and a verdict label — the set family's duplicate line printed `set-shrink-only` where its own docstring and the gate's frozen text said `set-covered` (the kind's word, not the mode's); the gate's exact-string check refused it on the shadow's first run. Neither reached a tracked file; the second is in this readout and the history, not a gotcha (as at bbx-7).

## What it rests on

The touched gates run in the shadow (a git tree built from HEAD) to a file with their exits tested — `set_schema` 5 controls, `provenance` 5, `docset_fixture` 3, `docset_driver` 7, `compare_dispatch` 3, `expectation_kinds` 3, `controls`, `readout`, `close_sweeps` 3 — then the two close batteries below in the tree.

## What this green does NOT assert

- The suite over the fixture (step 4): the loop over the kinds, the kept-run rows `(scenario, kind)`, `NOT-EVALUATED (schema failed)` on a schema FAIL, `--freeze` for the shrink-only kind, the coverage NOTEs on the screen. Until then every verdict line above is printed by a gate calling `compare_check`, not by a suite run.
- Anything about a real document set: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it; the forms, guards and lexical classes are the fixture's (D35, D39, D40).
- The schema family on a second format or consumer: `tsv` is its one format and the fixture's artifact its one consumer until S4's JSON (BBX-25 unmet, stated in the gate's header).
- That a register row's class is true of its file; that a tracked file is the RIGHT file (tracked-ness is presence in a clone, nothing more).
- Executable controls (R29, S6): every `CONTROL FIRED` here is the gate's self-report.

  tier: that a gate reaching an instrument through a path the source regex does not match is seen: the depth and the regex are the limit

  thresholds: that a ruling id in [thresholds].rulings names a ruling that exists: any non-empty string is accepted here; the consumer's rulings-shape gate is where an id is checked
  thresholds: the values' fitness for any subject: 2 / 60 / 8 are bbh's ratified policy carried in the frame-driven profile (D23), not a measurement of anything here
  compare_dispatch: the suite's dispatch around this library (which file is read, how many runs, the .sha1 and .diverge kinds' own paths): S2 step 3
  compare_dispatch: anything about a real log or a real mask: every log is synthesized and the masks are strings the guard compares, never applied
  expectation_kinds: the content of any expectation file: only its extension and its stem are read here
  expectation_kinds: the suite's use of the dispositions (S2 step 3)
  provenance: that a row's class is TRUE of its file: the register is written by hand at the freeze; only completeness and the vocabulary are checked
  provenance: bbh's example register (markdown, a consumer list of classes): it is bbh's and F19 (S6) reads it under R11

  docset_fixture: the suite over the fixture: step 4

  docset_driver: prose, reasoning and causal claims in a document: only sentences in a declared form and the listed rows are claims
  docset_driver: the truth of the artifact itself: a document that agrees with a wrong artifact reads BOUND
  docset_driver: a nested document tree or a second artifact per set (DOCSET_VIEW is refused): S4 or a consumer's question
  set_schema: the suite's loop over the kinds, the kept-run rows (scenario, kind), NOT-EVALUATED on a schema FAIL and --freeze for the shrink-only kind: S3 step 4
  set_schema: anything about a real document set: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it

  census_recount: the truth of the not-recountable rows at their commits: they are named and counted, never run
  census_recount: that a hand-read citation says what its row claims: only that the line exists
  fidelity_bbh: anything about a suite, a comparator or an expectation: slice S2
  fidelity_bbh: bbh's example's correctness (G11 is bbh's to fix)
  fidelity_bbh: F15 unless BBX_FIDELITY_F15=1 was set for the run
  fidelity_bbh_s2: the kept suite run (--log) — bbh has none, so F12 diffs printed text only; the kept run's ground truth is gates/suite.sh

The two batteries before the G23 fix (`build/selftest_20260910T121903Z`, `…T122414Z`) were NOT GREEN — `PASS 21 FAIL 1`, `set_schema` "exit 0 after a shell error", controls already 72 / 72 — and are kept as the incident's evidence. **Sweeps:** `close_sweeps` GREEN and `rulings_shape` GREEN inside both batteries, re-run once more on the final tree after these ledgers were written (below). **Lineage untouched, by construction and by proof:** bbh baseline=10a82d2 tip=447e5d2 ahead=1 porcelain=0 (clones only, clean after); VampireSaved past the census (not re-measured by design); sms recorded=ecc5481 tip=ecc5481 ahead=0 — the tips as the recount summaries printed them are in the kept runs' `census_recount.log`.

**Retractions this sitting:** X10 (the plan's control placement; the plan corrected in its own commit `08c9fef`, BBX-19). **Incidents reviewed for learnings (R27, step 10):** three — G22 (the untracked truth logs, paid 1 shadow run: a mechanism built, `bbx.provenance` tracked-ness with its control, and the shadow-as-git-tree practice written down), the set family's duplicate label (paid 0, caught by the gate's frozen text in the shadow: the mechanism is the freeze), G23 (the backticked word, paid 2 close batteries: a practice — the shadow runs gates through `bin/bbx classify` — the mechanism exists and fired).

**What this close does NOT assert:** the suite over the fixture (step 4: no suite run has printed one of the new verdict lines or a coverage number on the screen; `coverage: docset_driver: 20/23` is still a gate's NOTE); a real document set (every expectation is `fixture` class, the forms and guards the fixture's); the schema family beyond one format and one consumer (BBX-25 unmet); that a tracked file is the right file; bbh's tip beyond the baseline; any platform but Darwin arm64 (R21); executable controls (R29, S6); and everything S2's slice readout listed.

# bbx-10 step 4 — the suite's kinds loop: the document-set kind runs end to end and its coverage reaches the screen (2026-09-10)

**The open.** GREEN on `46cbe6f` (`build/selftest_20260910_144737`: 22 gates, 72 / 72 controls, porcelain 0, tree unchanged; the two drift NOTEs: bbh one ahead at `447e5d2`, VampireSaved 22 ahead): the bbx-9 close re-derived. R21 the only open ruling.

## What changed

**Measured before anything was built (§1):** the plan's §5 row said a caller's `DOCSET_NONDET=1` reads NONDETERMINISTIC through the suite. It does not: the document-set profile lists the driver family in `hermetic_unset` (D33), so the suite scrubs it — the two runs were byte-identical and the suite stayed on its verdicts — while the driver called directly under it wrote two logs differing at the first token. The plan corrected in its own commit `35c8007` (BBX-19; retraction X11) before the gate asserted the corrected shape; the control now goes through a wrapper driver passed with `--driver`, and the scrub is proven in the same control. D33 stands: a salted token must never reach a freeze.

**Step 4, as planned (§8.4), built in a shadow tree first and moved into the tree once every touched gate was green there through `bin/bbx classify`:**

- `bin/bbx-run-suite` — the KINDS LOOP. Which loop a run gets is read off the kinds table, never configured: a table that carries the temporal family (the frame-driven profile's `masked` / `diverge`) gets bbh's loop unchanged — one kind per scenario, `masked` > `diverge` > `sha1`, the printed text bbh's — and F12 (17 suite pairs among the 104 fidelity pairings) is the control that nothing moved for bbh; any other table gets the kinds loop: every EVAL kind whose file exists is its own pairing, the `schema` family FIRST (the artifact's shape before any value, R34), then the table's order, the self-frozen `.sha1` last (E4). The printed shape is `<scenario> <kind> <verdict>` with the scenario named once; run-level lines (SKIP, PENDING, RUN-FAIL, NONDETERMINISTIC, NO-EXPECTATION) print as bbh prints them, kept with kind `-`. On a schema FAIL the scenario's value kinds print the suite's own `NOT-EVALUATED (schema failed)` — finding `pending`, counted as failures, no NOTE kept for the scenario (a coverage of a wrong shape is a number about nothing). The comparators that read the artifact get it through `compare_check`'s two trailing arguments: the scenario file and the SUBJECT FILE the fingerprint resolved (`bbx.fingerprint --path`, new: the file the identity was computed from — proven equal to the driver's own resolver on the fixture). After a scenario's pairings `[suite].log_summary` (D43) prints the log's own numbers at column 0 — the document-set profile's `bbx.docset summary`, kind-blind none, so the frame-driven text is untouched — and every `NOTE:` line the scenario printed is kept in `notes.tsv` (D44). `--freeze` under the kinds loop rewrites the shrink-only kind from the run (`bbx.compare_set --freeze`: the `[spec]` kept, one table per covered row in extraction order — on the fixture the rewritten file equals the generator's byte for byte) and leaves every other authored kind alone (`authored .<kind> expectation — not self-frozen`); the `.sha1` self-freeze runs ONLY for a scenario with no authored kind, so `logs/<name>.log` — where an exact kind's truth log lives — is never overwritten beside a `.truth`: the bbx-9 HANDOFF hazard closed by construction, and proven in both directions.
- `lib/py/bbx/finding.py` — `NOT-EVALUATED` → `pending` (E2's disposition) and `authored ` as the prefix (bbh's `.masked` line is one of them); no new word.
- `lib/py/bbx/readout.py` — the suite screen counts scenarios and pairings apart, prints `coverage:` per scenario and every other key as a note from `notes.tsv`, meets BBX-14 over pairings `(scenario, kind)`, and lists the DRIVER's `NOT-ASSERTED` lines under "what this green does NOT assert" (RO2 — bbh's fake driver declares none, and the screen says so). `gates/readout.sh`'s three frozen lines moved with it.
- `gates/docset_suite.sh` (portable, 6 controls, 17 suite runs, ~55 s): the tree's fixture GREEN with all 29 printed lines frozen and the 12 verdict lines classified `pass`; the kept run's 12 rows keyed `(scenario, kind)` in the loop's order, the 15 notes, `loop=kinds`; the second run byte-identical and the readout over both exit 0 with the eight screen lines checked; the six controls below; the `.sha1` beside the authored kinds as a fifth pairing; NO-EXPECTATION; the fixture's every file checksummed before and after (untouched).
- Headers: the four blind-spot lines that said "S3 step 4" now name `gates/docset_suite.sh`; `gates/suite.sh`'s two that had named "S2 step 4" since bbx-4 (G24) name `gates/readout.sh` and `gates/provenance.sh`. `docs/defaults.md` D43, D44. `gates/portable.txt` +1.

**One thing the plan's §6 words differently from the screen:** §6 says `comparator classes: exact / set / schema`; the screen's class column carries the spec's `class` field — `exact, multiset, schema` — because `set` is the family and `multiset` its one class (as `masked` is the kind and `flicker` the class). Not a retraction: the sentence names families and the screen names classes; recorded here so the next reader is not surprised.

## What it rests on

Every touched gate run in the shadow (a git tree built from HEAD) to a file and read through `bin/bbx classify` — the two static ones with `BBX_BBH_HOME` — then the two close batteries below in the tree. The kinds loop's first green was read from its printed values (the 29 lines above) before the gate froze them (BBX-2).

## What this green does NOT assert

- Anything about a real document set: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it; the forms, guards and lexical classes are the fixture's (D35, D39, D40). `PASSed on a real pairing: none` is the screen's own sentence.
- A second consumer of the kinds loop: one profile drives it (S4's command-line kind is the second; BBX-25 unmet, stated in the gate's header). The schema family still has one format and one consumer.
- The readout screen beyond the lines the gate checks (the verdict, the pairings, the coverage and note lines, BBX-14, the driver's blind spots): the rest is `gates/readout.sh`'s.
- That a register row's class is true of its file; that a tracked file is the right file.
- The slice's own accounting (step 5): families per kind, controls declared / fired per gate, provenance classes, defaults rows, the first shared-vs-kind-specific file census, and what the slice's green does not assert — the readout that lets the maintainer rule S3 done (§7).
- Executable controls (R29, S6): every `CONTROL FIRED` here is the gate's self-report.

  thresholds: that a ruling id in [thresholds].rulings names a ruling that exists: any non-empty string is accepted here; the consumer's rulings-shape gate is where an id is checked
  thresholds: the values' fitness for any subject: 2 / 60 / 8 are bbh's ratified policy carried in the frame-driven profile (D23), not a measurement of anything here
  compare_dispatch: the suite's dispatch around this library (which file is read, how many runs, the .sha1 and .diverge kinds' own paths): S2 step 3
  compare_dispatch: anything about a real log or a real mask: every log is synthesized and the masks are strings the guard compares, never applied
  expectation_kinds: the content of any expectation file: only its extension and its stem are read here
  expectation_kinds: the suite's use of the dispositions (S2 step 3)
  provenance: that a row's class is TRUE of its file: the register is written by hand at the freeze; only completeness and the vocabulary are checked
  provenance: bbh's example register (markdown, a consumer list of classes): it is bbh's and F19 (S6) reads it under R11
  docset_fixture: that any claim in the fixture is BOUND, MISMATCH or anything else (gates/docset_driver.sh and gates/set_schema.sh): only that the files are the generator's, the design is chiral, and the register is complete and tracked
  docset_fixture: the suite over the fixture: gates/docset_suite.sh
  docset_driver: the verdict text of the exact, set and schema comparators (S3 step 3): the truth is compared here with cmp and diff
  docset_driver: the suite over the fixture (identity, the kinds loop, the coverage NOTE on the screen): gates/docset_suite.sh
  docset_driver: prose, reasoning and causal claims in a document: only sentences in a declared form and the listed rows are claims
  docset_driver: the truth of the artifact itself: a document that agrees with a wrong artifact reads BOUND
  docset_driver: a nested document tree or a second artifact per set (DOCSET_VIEW is refused): S4 or a consumer's question
  set_schema: the suite's loop over the kinds, the kept-run rows (scenario, kind), NOT-EVALUATED on a schema FAIL and --freeze for the shrink-only kind: gates/docset_suite.sh
  set_schema: anything about a real document set: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it
  set_schema: the schema family on a second format or a second consumer (S4's JSON): tsv is its one format and the fixture's artifact its one consumer (BBX-25 unmet, stated)
  docset_suite: anything about a real document set: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it; the forms, guards and lexical classes are the fixture's (D35, D39, D40)
  docset_suite: bbh's precedence loop and its printed text: gates/fidelity_bbh_s2.sh (F12) and gates/suite.sh
  docset_suite: the readout screen beyond the lines checked here (the verdict, the pairings, the coverage and note lines, BBX-14, the driver's blind spots): gates/readout.sh
  docset_suite: a second consumer of the kinds loop (S4's command-line kind): one profile drives it here (BBX-25 unmet, stated)
  census_recount: the truth of the not-recountable rows at their commits: they are named and counted, never run
  census_recount: that a hand-read citation says what its row claims: only that the line exists
  fidelity_bbh: anything about a suite, a comparator or an expectation: slice S2
  fidelity_bbh: bbh's example's correctness (G11 is bbh's to fix)
  fidelity_bbh: F15 unless BBX_FIDELITY_F15=1 was set for the run
  fidelity_bbh_s2: the kept suite run (--log) — bbh has none, so F12 diffs printed text only; the kept run's ground truth is gates/suite.sh
  fidelity_bbh_s2: anything about a real subject: F17's and F16b's inputs are synthesized, F16a's are the fake machine's (fixture class); the shapes are the lineage's paid-for cases and nothing else
  fidelity_bbh_s2: bbh's correctness: identical output on both sides is fidelity, not truth
  suite: any driver but the fake: a MAME or FBNeo driver is bbh's and untested here (bbh's own F8 rows)
  suite: the .sha1 kind's evidence: it is `self` class by construction (E4) and the register that says so is gates/provenance.sh's
  suite: the readout's reading of a kept suite run: gates/readout.sh reads the suite screen, gates/docset_suite.sh its coverage lines
  gates declaring no blind spot: 0   (a gate nobody has asked what its green leaves out)
```

# CLOSE — bbx-10 (2026-09-10)

**Close measurements.** Green first, twice, kept (`build/selftest_20260910T130844Z`, `build/selftest_20260910T131501Z`): `PASS 23 SKIP 0 FAIL 0 MISSING 0` (23 gates); `controls fired 78 / declared 78`; `ok: no tracked file changed during the run` in both; BBX-14 met (23 gates, 0 verdict differences at one HEAD). **Sweeps:** `close_sweeps` and `rulings_shape` inside both batteries, re-run once more on the final tree after these ledgers were written (the commit message quotes the two verdicts). **Lineage untouched, by construction and by proof:** bbh baseline=10a82d2 tip=447e5d2 ahead=1 porcelain=0 (clones only, clean after); VampireSaved past the census (not re-measured by design); the tips as the recount summaries printed them are in the kept runs' `census_recount.log`.

**Retractions this sitting:** X11 (the plan's §5 nondeterminism row; the plan corrected in its own commit `35c8007`, BBX-19). **Incidents reviewed for learnings (R27, step 10):** G24 (two blind-spot lines naming "S2 step 4" as their future since bbx-4; paid 0; G19's mechanism, now with two instances, and a practice: a blind-spot line names the gate that will close it); the new gate's two defects caught in the shadow (paid 1 shadow run; the shadow-through-the-classifier practice is the mechanism and it fired).

**What this close does NOT assert:** a real document set (every expectation is `fixture` class; the screen says `PASSed on a real pairing: none`); a second consumer of the kinds loop or of the schema family (BBX-25 unmet, stated in `gates/docset_suite.sh`); the slice's own accounting and the file census (step 5); bbh's tip beyond the baseline; any platform but Darwin arm64 (R21); executable controls (R29, S6); and everything S2's slice readout listed.

## What changed

**No tool was written** (`docs/plans/S3.md` §8.5; the handoff's "no tool is written in step 5"). The sitting produced: this readout; `docs/census/bbx_files.md`, the first shared-vs-kind-specific FILE CENSUS, measured at runtime in a shadow tree by a scratchpad generator that is reproduced verbatim in the census's §C and run by hand — **measured once, not gated**: the gate is S4's plan to decide; the plan's §6 screen line corrected in its own commit before this readout quoted it (BBX-19; X12: `fixture 15`, not `fixture 10`); G25 (the instrument's first version ended every header, caught by `gates/docset_suite.sh`, paid 1 shadow gate run). `docs/plans/S3.md` §5's "Fourteen controls, in five gates" was the design; the measure is 21 controls in 4 gates (the fifth gate was X10's).

## The slice readout (`docs/slices.md` "What a slice's readout must carry"; CLAUDE.md §7)

**3. Fidelity** (`build/selftest_20260910T134014Z/fidelity_bbh_s2.log`, `fidelity_bbh.log`; the plain clone of bbh at `10a82d2`, R20; the clone clean after): F12+F16+F17 `104 pairs, 0 differ` (F17 52, F16 35, F12 17); F13a–e and F14/F14f 21 pairs identical; F15 not run (opt-in). **4. Every frozen expectation's provenance class** — the document-set fixture's register `fixture/docset/expected/PROVENANCE.toml`, 16 rows, written by the generator: `fixture` 15 (the 12 expectation files — 3 scenarios × `.truth` `.claims` `.covered` `.schema` — and the 3 truth logs) and `registry` 1 (`registry.tsv`); complete both ways, inside R11's vocabulary and every file git-tracked (`gates/docset_fixture.sh` runs `bbx.provenance` over the real register; `gates/provenance.sh` proves the three refusals). The frame-driven kind's example carries no register (bbh's; F19 is S6) and the screen says `none registered` for it.

**5. Every default the slice introduced, in the register** (`docs/defaults.md` D33–D44, 12 rows, each written before first use — `gates/close_sweeps.sh` fails a cited id with no row): `principled` 6 (D33 the profile, D36 chirality, D38 the two strings, D42 the NOTE keys, D43 `log_summary`, D44 `notes.tsv`); `principled (ruled)` 2 (D37 the status vocabulary R31/R33, D41 the schema types R34); `arbitrary` 2 (D35 the fixture's three sentence forms, D39 the lexical classes and record key); split 2 (D34 `scenario_ext`: arbitrary kind-blind `rpl` / principled `claims`; D40 the guards: arbitrary regexes / principled that an unlisted absence is loud). **6. Comparator families per kind** (`python3 -m bbx.expectations kinds` under each profile's config; `docs/census/bbx_files.md` A3, A4): document-set **3** — `truth` exact (by index), `claims` and `covered` set (multiset both ways; shrink-only), `schema` — the design target A = 3 of `docs/generality.md` met; frame-driven **1** — `masked` and `diverge`, temporal; the kind-blind `sha1` (exact, disposition N/A) under both. B = 4 (the tolerant-numeric family) and the second consumer of `schema` are S4's.

**7. The first shared-vs-kind-specific FILE CENSUS** (`docs/census/bbx_files.md`: the method, every row, the generator; measured once, not gated). Runtime traces of what each of the 23 gates executes, in a shadow tree built from HEAD; a gate's kind derived from its trace (F: a temporal-family module or bbh's tree; D: a document-set module or driver; K: neither), never from its vocabulary. Universe: 37 tracked files under `lib/`, `bin/`, `drivers/`.

| files executed by … | count | the rows |
|---|---|---|
| gates of BOTH kinds — the abstraction, proved by two instances | **11** | `bin/bbx-run-suite`, `lib/sh/compare.sh`, `lib/sh/config.sh`, `lib/sh/expectation_kinds.sh`, `config.py`, `expectations.py`, `finding.py`, `fingerprint.py`, `logfmt.py`, `toml_subset.py`, `__init__.py` |
| frame-driven gates only (no kernel gate) | **7** | the temporal family: `compare_flicker` `compare_window` `compare_composite` `check_diverge` `propose_temporal` `thresholds`; `_mklog` (the synthetic-log helper) |
| frame-driven AND kernel gates, never a document-set gate | **5** | `bin/bbx-run-static`, `bin/bbx-run-sweep`, `tier.py`, `lib/sh/classify.sh`, `lib/sh/registry.sh` — the static and sweep runners: the document-set fixture has no gate battery of its own to run them over |
| document-set gates only (no kernel gate) | **7** | the document-set family: `docset.py`, `compare_exact` `compare_set` `compare_schema`, `drivers/docset.sh`; `bin/bbx` (the dispatcher — ONE gate, `set_schema`, through `bbx compare`); `sha1.py` (one gate, the `.sha1`-beside-authored-kinds pairing) |
| document-set AND kernel gates, never a frame-driven gate | **3** | `controls.py`, `provenance.py`, `readout.py` — `docset_suite` reads a kept run through the readout; the frame-driven gates diff printed text (F12: bbh has no kept run) and `gates/readout.sh` reads a synthetic frame-shaped run |

| no gate | **0** | — |

Gates by derived kind: K 13, F 6 (`compare_dispatch`, `temporal`, `thresholds`, `fidelity_bbh`, `fidelity_bbh_s2`, `suite`), D 4 (`docset_fixture`, `docset_driver`, `set_schema`, `docset_suite`), both 0. Files executed by exactly one gate: 6 (`bin/bbx`, `bin/bbx-classify`, `close_sweeps.py`, `recount.py`, `rulings_shape.py`, `sha1.py`); by 15 gates or more: 3 (`config.py`, `toml_subset.py`, `__init__.py`). Two runs of the generator at one HEAD: every gate's file set byte-identical (23 of 23, `cmp`), the analysis identical — BBX-14 met for the census (its §D). What the census reads: the 11 shared files are the contracts (the suite loop, the dispatcher library, the config layers, the kinds table, the finding vocabulary, the identity, the log format, the register format) and every one is executed by both kinds' gates — BBX-25 met for the S3 abstraction by measurement, not by the plan's list; the 7 + 7 kind-specific files are exactly the two comparator families with their driver and log helper, as the design meant. The two "+kernel" rows are the census's finding for S4: `bin/bbx` is executed by one gate, and the static and sweep runners by no document-set gate — facts about the gates' reach, stated, not verdicts.

**8. Rules re-anchored this slice, by incident, with its price** — none PROMOTED (CLAUDE.md §4 still marks all 30 `[inherited]`; edits need R16; the promotion is S5). Re-anchored in fact during S3: G20 → BBX-5 (1 gate run, shadow); G21 → §1, BBX-16 (0: the gate's first run measured it); G22 → BBX-10, §1 (1 shadow run); G23 → BBX-1 (2 close batteries, ~12 min); G24 → BBX-10 (0); G25 → BBX-5, BBX-16, R30 (1 shadow gate run, 86 s). Six incidents, four caught by a gate or the shadow before a number was read off them.

## What the slice's green does NOT assert

- Anything about a REAL document set: every expectation is `fixture` class (the suite screen's own sentence: `PASSed on a real pairing: none`); the sentence forms, guards and lexical classes are the fixture's (D35, D39, D40) and their detector is the first real consumer.
- A second consumer of the kinds loop, of the `schema` family (one format, TSV) or of the set family: BBX-25 unmet for each, stated in `gates/docset_suite.sh` and `gates/set_schema.sh`; S4's command-line kind is the second consumer by design.

The plan's header says it was written while S3's ruling is pending: if S3 is ruled not done, the condition named is the next sitting's first task (BBX-26) and the plan waits. The S4 row of `docs/slices.md` points at the plan; DECISIONS.md's and STATE.md's open-rulings lines carry R35–R40 (`gates/rulings_shape.sh` caught the mismatch before the commit — `open-line-mismatch listed=R21 actual=R21,R35,…,R40` — and reads `entries=41 open=7 answered=34` after it). what this green does NOT assert (declared by each gate's header):
  classify: that a verdict word printed by a gate outside the runner is read at all: the classifier reads exit status first, then the log; a PASS printed after a non-zero exit is FAIL by design
  config: the meaning of a consumer's keys: only that the layers resolve (consumer over kind profile over kind-blind default) and that dumps are stable
  tier: that a gate reaching an instrument through a path the source regex does not match is seen: the depth and the regex are the limit
  static_runner: the sweep runner or any gate that needs an instrument: this is the pre-commit chain only
  static_runner: runtimes as anything but this host under this load
  controls: that a control is RIGHT — only that a declared control fired and an undeclared one is red (docs/controls.md)
  sweep_runner: speed-up on a real consumer: the queue is measured on stub gates with sleeps, not on an instrument-tier suite
  sweep_runner: a gate that escapes its clone by an absolute path: clone-per-slot pins the cwd, nothing more
  sweep_runner: portability beyond macOS: mkfifo and exec 8<> are POSIX, not yet run on Linux or WSL
  fingerprint: the identity of any artifact that is not a single file: the kind-blind fingerprint is file-sha1 (D16)
  rulings_shape: the prose of a ruling: only its heading, its answer line and its DECISIONS row are read
  readout: that a declared blind spot is true or complete: the screen prints what the header says
  readout: the sweep runner's runs: only bbx-run-static --log and bbx-run-suite --log are read
  readout: that a register row's class is true of its file: the suite screen prints what the register says (gates/provenance.sh keeps it complete and inside the vocabulary)
  close_sweeps: that every corrected claim has a register row: the register is written by hand at the correction (a claim nobody registered is not swept)
  close_sweeps: step 9 of the close (the lineage untouched): that is the recount's clone and the fidelity gate's proof, not this gate
  temporal: anything about a real log: every shape here is synthesized (fixture class); the classes' fitness for a consumer is that consumer's ratification
  temporal: the dispatcher's spec line and the suite's dispatch (S2 steps 2 and 3): the comparators are called directly
  temporal: that the thresholds are right for any subject: they are the frame-driven profile's (D23) and a consumer's override needs R25's ruling row (gates/thresholds.sh)
  thresholds: that a ruling id in [thresholds].rulings names a ruling that exists: any non-empty string is accepted here; the consumer's rulings-shape gate is where an id is checked
  thresholds: the values' fitness for any subject: 2 / 60 / 8 are bbh's ratified policy carried in the frame-driven profile (D23), not a measurement of anything here
  compare_dispatch: the suite's dispatch around this library (which file is read, how many runs, the .sha1 and .diverge kinds' own paths): S2 step 3
  compare_dispatch: anything about a real log or a real mask: every log is synthesized and the masks are strings the guard compares, never applied
  expectation_kinds: the content of any expectation file: only its extension and its stem are read here
  expectation_kinds: the suite's use of the dispositions (S2 step 3)
  provenance: that a row's class is TRUE of its file: the register is written by hand at the freeze; only completeness and the vocabulary are checked
  provenance: bbh's example register (markdown, a consumer list of classes): it is bbh's and F19 (S6) reads it under R11
  docset_fixture: that any claim in the fixture is BOUND, MISMATCH or anything else (gates/docset_driver.sh and gates/set_schema.sh): only that the files are the generator's, the design is chiral, and the register is complete and tracked
  docset_fixture: the suite over the fixture: gates/docset_suite.sh
  docset_driver: the verdict text of the exact, set and schema comparators (S3 step 3): the truth is compared here with cmp and diff
  docset_driver: the suite over the fixture (identity, the kinds loop, the coverage NOTE on the screen): gates/docset_suite.sh
  docset_driver: prose, reasoning and causal claims in a document: only sentences in a declared form and the listed rows are claims
  docset_driver: the truth of the artifact itself: a document that agrees with a wrong artifact reads BOUND
  docset_driver: a nested document tree or a second artifact per set (DOCSET_VIEW is refused): S4 or a consumer's question
  set_schema: the suite's loop over the kinds, the kept-run rows (scenario, kind), NOT-EVALUATED on a schema FAIL and --freeze for the shrink-only kind: gates/docset_suite.sh
  set_schema: anything about a real document set: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it
  set_schema: the schema family on a second format or a second consumer (S4's JSON): tsv is its one format and the fixture's artifact its one consumer (BBX-25 unmet, stated)
  docset_suite: anything about a real document set: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it; the forms, guards and lexical classes are the fixture's (D35, D39, D40)
  docset_suite: bbh's precedence loop and its printed text: gates/fidelity_bbh_s2.sh (F12) and gates/suite.sh
  docset_suite: the readout screen beyond the lines checked here (the verdict, the pairings, the coverage and note lines, BBX-14, the driver's blind spots): gates/readout.sh
  docset_suite: a second consumer of the kinds loop (S4's command-line kind): one profile drives it here (BBX-25 unmet, stated)
  cli_fixture: the driver (S4 step 2): the tool is run here DIRECTLY by the generator's tool-check; no log in D47's grammar is produced by anything but the generator, so the truth logs describe the design, not a run
  cli_fixture: the comparators over the new kinds (`unordered`, `schema` json, `band`: S4 step 3) and the suite over the fixture (step 4): only that the files are the generator's, the design is chiral, the tool matches it, and the register is complete and tracked
  census_recount: the truth of the not-recountable rows at their commits: they are named and counted, never run
  census_recount: that a hand-read citation says what its row claims: only that the line exists
  fidelity_bbh: anything about a suite, a comparator or an expectation: slice S2
  fidelity_bbh: bbh's example's correctness (G11 is bbh's to fix)
  fidelity_bbh: F15 unless BBX_FIDELITY_F15=1 was set for the run
  fidelity_bbh_s2: the kept suite run (--log) — bbh has none, so F12 diffs printed text only; the kept run's ground truth is gates/suite.sh
  fidelity_bbh_s2: anything about a real subject: F17's and F16b's inputs are synthesized, F16a's are the fake machine's (fixture class); the shapes are the lineage's paid-for cases and nothing else
  fidelity_bbh_s2: bbh's correctness: identical output on both sides is fidelity, not truth
  suite: any driver but the fake: a MAME or FBNeo driver is bbh's and untested here (bbh's own F8 rows)
  suite: the .sha1 kind's evidence: it is `self` class by construction (E4) and the register that says so is gates/provenance.sh's
  suite: the readout's reading of a kept suite run: gates/readout.sh reads the suite screen, gates/docset_suite.sh its coverage lines
  gates declaring no blind spot: 0   (a gate nobody has asked what its green leaves out)
```

## What changed

**The plan first (BBX-19), in its own commit `ddeb0aa`.** Five wordings of `docs/plans/S4.md` the built fixture differs from, each measured on the shadow build before the tree saw it: the tool lives at `fixture/fakecli/subject/fakecli.py`, in its own directory, so the file-sha1 whole-set key (`dir_sha1` over the tool's directory, not recursive) covers the subject and never its generator or config (X14, also `docs/generality.md`); 9 options — `--sleep <s>`, needed by §5's timeout control, was missing from the row (X15); 7 refusal paths, counted by the generator, not the 2 the scenarios exercise (X16); `CLI_PATH` is the suite's search-path variable and not in `hermetic_unset`, D33's shape (X17); 5 commands — `list --unordered` is an option (X18); `stdin` is an array of lines because the TOML subset refuses `\n` in a basic string, `stdin_file` relative to the scenario file's directory (X19). And one sequencing note without a retraction: `log_summary` joins the profile with the driver at step 2, as D43 joined the document-set profile at S3 step 4.

**The profile** `command-line` in `lib/py/bbx/config.py` KINDS (D45): instrument word "tool"; portable (no `static_needs_env`, no tier pattern); `scenario_ext = "cli"` (D34 amended), `driver = "cli"`, `rompath_env = input_env = CLI_PATH`, `hermetic_unset` the driver family `CLI_NONDET` / `CLI_TIMEOUT` / `CLI_KEEP_ENV`; `[fingerprint]` file-sha1 over `{set}.py`; the kinds table the kind-blind three plus `truth` (exact), `unordered` (set), `schema` (schema), `band` (tolerant-numeric) — the exact, set and schema families each gain their second consumer on paper here and in code at step 3; no temporal family, no thresholds.

**The token vocabulary** `lib/py/bbx/cli.py` (D47), the S3 shape: one writer of every token, imported by the generator now and grown into the driver's core at step 2 — `0 exit:<n>` first; `<i> line:<sha1>` per stdout line, or under `fields = "json"` `<i> field:<name>:<sha1-of-canonical-JSON>` per key in sorted order and `field:<name>:band` for a band field; `<i> err:<sha1>`; `<i> file:<name>:<sha1-of-bytes>`; `END <n>` with n the last index.

**The fixture** `fixture/fakecli/` (BBX's third consumer; `bbx.toml` kind `command-line`, `default_set = "fakecli"`, `replays_dir = "scenarios"`): `mkfakecli.py` writes everything from the design and re-derives under `--check` — `subject/fakecli.py` (the TOOL, generated from a template with the design table injected: 5 commands `list show stat join report`, 9 options, 7 refusal paths in cliguard's shape, `FAKECLI_SALT` the one environment variable it declares), nine `.cli` scenarios (D46), nine `truth` files with their truth logs written through `bbx.cli` from the design (never by running the tool), `02_unordered.unordered` (the multiset of the unordered listing, one bare table per row with `line` and `sha1`), `03_show_json.schema` (five keys sorted, `str str int int list`, the list with `items_op`/`items_n`; D49), `04_band.band` (`size` in `[236, 270]`, measured by the generator over seeds 1..9 of the design's `231 + (seed × 29) mod 41`; D48), `expected/registry.tsv` (the whole-set key → `fixture`) and `PROVENANCE.toml` (22 rows: 21 `fixture`, 1 `registry`). Chiral by construction (D50): D36's ten predicates plus the rank order neither the name order nor its reverse, no name, id or weight shared with `fixture/docset`'s design (read from `mkdocset.py` through the harness root — measured), the join stdin in neither sorted order. **The tool-check:** `--check` also runs the tree's tool for every scenario under D6's clean environment in a TMPDIR working directory and compares exit, stdout, stderr and emitted files with what the design says — the fixture's own BBX-5 pairing (the design the author, the tool the artifact), so the truth logs are known to describe a tool that exists.

**The gate** `gates/cli_fixture.sh` (portable; the 24th gate), four controls each on a copy with the perturbation proven applied: `edited-value` (one weight in the copy's tool → `DIFFERS subject/fakecli.py`), `symmetric-fixture` (two equal weights → `weights-distinct` named), `tool-drifts-from-design` (a shadow generator with the list line's format changed, the copy regenerated from it so every file equals that generator's, then `--check` → `TOOL-DIFFERS 01_list: stdout line 1 differs`), `unknown-kind-under-profile` (a `.masked` beside a `.cli` → `UNKNOWN-KIND`, non-zero: R23's detector on the fourth profile). It also proves every TOML-subset file the fixture introduces is read by the subset parser (22 files, 0 refused — R40's caveat measured, no new TSV but the registry the kernel already had), that the registry row is `bbx.fingerprint --set-key` over `subject/`, that the register is complete both ways and every registered file is tracked by git (G22).

**Built in a shadow tree first** (`git archive HEAD`, `git init`, the gate through the classifier). The shadow caught three defects of the new gate and generator before the tree saw them: the gate's two `sed` perturbations were written in the design's double-quoted form while the generated tool carries Python's repr (single quotes) — both controls read "the perturbation did not apply"; a COPIED generator resolved `fixture/docset/mkdocset.py` relative to itself, and a copy under TMPDIR has no sibling (a traceback under the `tool-drifts-from-design` control); the fix's parent depth off by one (`lib/` instead of the root). Then the tree caught what the shadow could not: with the fixture files copied in but not yet staged, `bbx.provenance` refused all 22 rows as naming files git does not track — the G22 guard, firing on the mirror case (the shadow had committed them). Staged, PASS. Paid: four gate runs of ~3 s and one battery aborted at its first minute. Learnings (R27): two hazard lines in `HANDOFF.md`.
