## What it rests on

The gate run in the tree through the classifier with its exit tested (`cli_fixture` 4 controls), the eight neighbouring gates the same way in the shadow (`config`, `expectation_kinds`, `fingerprint`, `controls`, `readout`, `tier`, `provenance`, `static_runner`: PASS); the generator's own `--check` on the tree; `close_sweeps` PASS (`retractions=19 retraction_hits=0 deferrals=0 defaults_rows=50 citations=367 unresolved=0`) and `rulings_shape` PASS on the tree; the two close batteries below.

## What this green does NOT assert

- The driver (step 2): the tool is run here DIRECTLY by the generator's tool-check; no log in D47's grammar is produced by anything but the generator, so the truth logs describe the DESIGN. Step 2's gate compares the driver's log with each truth log byte for byte, and hashes one point in `sh` beside `bbx.cli` (a lineage-independent check on the one writer, BBX-15).
- The comparators over the new kinds — `unordered` through `compare_set.py` (whose row model is the document-set's today), `schema` with `format = "json"`, `band` — and their verdict text (step 3); the suite over the fixture, the identity refusal, `--freeze`'s refusal to widen, `NOTE: band-fields` on the screen (step 4); the adapters and the self subject (step 5); the file census as a gate (step 6).
- That a band is right for anything but the fixture: `[236, 270]` is class `fixture` (R11), a measurement of a designed function.
- The fitness of D46's grammar or D47's vocabulary for a real tool: the first real consumer is the detector (BBX-24, BBX-25). A tool that is a directory, a non-UTF-8 stdout, an emitted TREE, a fractional band (§9 of the plan).
- D9's default kind stays `frame-driven` with a fourth profile in the table (the plan's choice); a config for any other kind names it.
- Any platform but Darwin arm64 (R21). Executable controls (R29, S6): every `CONTROL FIRED` here is the gate's self-report.

# CLOSE — bbx-13 (2026-09-10)

**Close measurements.** Green first, twice, kept (`build/selftest_20260910T171631Z`, `build/selftest_20260910T172316Z`): `PASS 24 SKIP 0 FAIL 0 MISSING 0`; `controls fired 82 / declared 82; gates with no declaration: 0; red: 0`; `ok: no tracked file changed during the run` on both; BBX-14 met on the screen above (24 gates, 0 verdict differences at one HEAD). Fidelity F12–F17 unchanged on the plain clone at `10a82d2`; bbh's tip `447e5d2` one past the baseline (a NOTE); VampireSaved past its census (a NOTE). `close_sweeps` PASS (`retractions=19 retraction_hits=0 deferrals=0 defaults_rows=50 citations=367 unresolved=0`) and `rulings_shape` PASS on the final tree. Both batteries ran on `ddeb0aa` with the step-1 files staged (porcelain 41 on the screen: the build and the ledgers this commit carries).

**Retractions this sitting:** X14–X19, all the plan's (BBX-19), each corrected before step 1 landed. **Defaults:** D45–D50, D34 amended. **Incidents reviewed for learnings (R27, step 10):** four events caught before the commit, none filed as a gotcha, priced in the step-1 section above; two learnings written as hazards in `HANDOFF.md` (a generator locates a sibling fixture through the harness root; the new gate runs in the tree before the battery).

**What this close does NOT assert:** anything the step-1 section's blind spots name — the driver, the comparators over the new kinds, the suite over the fixture, the adapters, the census gate (steps 2–6); bbh's tip beyond the baseline; any platform but Darwin arm64 (R21); executable controls (R29, S6).

```
  expectation_kinds: the suite's use of the dispositions (S2 step 3)
  provenance: that a row's class is TRUE of its file: the register is written by hand at the freeze; only completeness and the vocabulary are checked
  provenance: bbh's example register (markdown, a consumer list of classes): it is bbh's and F19 (S6) reads it under R11
  docset_fixture: that any claim in the fixture is BOUND, MISMATCH or anything else (gates/docset_driver.sh and gates/set_schema.sh): only that the files are the generator's, the design is chiral, and the register is complete and tracked
  docset_fixture: the suite over the fixture: gates/docset_suite.sh
  docset_driver: the verdict text of the exact, set and schema comparators (S3 step 3): the truth is compared here with cmp and diff
  docset_driver: the suite over the fixture (identity, the kinds loop, the coverage NOTE on the screen): gates/docset_suite.sh
  docset_driver: prose, reasoning and causal claims in a document: only sentences in a declared form and the listed rows are claims
  docset_driver: the truth of the artifact itself: a document that agrees with a wrong artifact reads BOUND
  docset_driver: a nested document tree or a second artifact per set (DOCSET_VIEW is refused): S4 or a consumer's question
  set_schema: the suite's loop over the kinds, the kept-run rows (scenario, kind), NOT-EVALUATED on a schema FAIL and --freeze for the shrink-only kind: gates/docset_suite.sh
  set_schema: anything about a real document set: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it
  set_schema: the schema family on a second format or a second consumer (S4's JSON): tsv is its one format and the fixture's artifact its one consumer (BBX-25 unmet, stated)
  docset_suite: anything about a real document set: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it; the forms, guards and lexical classes are the fixture's (D35, D39, D40)
  docset_suite: bbh's precedence loop and its printed text: gates/fidelity_bbh_s2.sh (F12) and gates/suite.sh
  docset_suite: the readout screen beyond the lines checked here (the verdict, the pairings, the coverage and note lines, BBX-14, the driver's blind spots): gates/readout.sh
  docset_suite: a second consumer of the kinds loop (S4's command-line kind): one profile drives it here (BBX-25 unmet, stated)
  cli_fixture: the driver (S4 step 2): the tool is run here DIRECTLY by the generator's tool-check; no log in D47's grammar is produced by anything but the generator, so the truth logs describe the design, not a run
  cli_fixture: the comparators over the new kinds (`unordered`, `schema` json, `band`: S4 step 3) and the suite over the fixture (step 4): only that the files are the generator's, the design is chiral, the tool matches it, and the register is complete and tracked
  cli_driver: the verdict text of the exact, set, schema and band comparators over these logs (S4 step 3): the truth is compared here with cmp and diff
  cli_driver: the suite over the fixture (identity, the kinds loop, RUN-FAIL on a crash, the band NOTE on the screen): S4 step 4
  cli_driver: performance, behaviour on inputs outside the scenarios, and anything the tool wrote that the scenario did not declare
  cli_driver: a tool that is a directory, a non-UTF-8 output, an emitted TREE, a fractional band (docs/plans/S4.md §9): a consumer's question, refused or discarded here, never measured
  census_recount: the truth of the not-recountable rows at their commits: they are named and counted, never run
  census_recount: that a hand-read citation says what its row claims: only that the line exists
  fidelity_bbh: anything about a suite, a comparator or an expectation: slice S2
  fidelity_bbh: bbh's example's correctness (G11 is bbh's to fix)
  fidelity_bbh: F15 unless BBX_FIDELITY_F15=1 was set for the run
  fidelity_bbh_s2: the kept suite run (--log) — bbh has none, so F12 diffs printed text only; the kept run's ground truth is gates/suite.sh
  fidelity_bbh_s2: anything about a real subject: F17's and F16b's inputs are synthesized, F16a's are the fake machine's (fixture class); the shapes are the lineage's paid-for cases and nothing else
  fidelity_bbh_s2: bbh's correctness: identical output on both sides is fidelity, not truth
  suite: any driver but the fake: a MAME or FBNeo driver is bbh's and untested here (bbh's own F8 rows)
  suite: the .sha1 kind's evidence: it is `self` class by construction (E4) and the register that says so is gates/provenance.sh's
  suite: the readout's reading of a kept suite run: gates/readout.sh reads the suite screen, gates/docset_suite.sh its coverage lines
  gates declaring no blind spot: 0   (a gate nobody has asked what its green leaves out)
```

## What changed

**The plan needed no correction for this step (BBX-19 asked and answered):** every wording of `docs/plans/S4.md` §3 "D1–D5", "O5", "O6" and §5's driver rows is what was built. Where the plan left a detail open, the readout says how it was decided, each a row in `docs/defaults.md` (D51–D53) or a sentence in the module's header: a tool killed by a signal has no exit status, so the crash log carries no `0 exit:` line — its points, then `CRASH signal:<n>:<NAME>`, then `END-CRASH <n>` (D53); `<set>.py` runs under the harness's own python3 (`sys.executable`), never one the pinned PATH would find, and an executable `<set>` runs as itself (D52); "`env -i`" is the subprocess started with exactly D6's dict plus the `[env]` table — the same effect, and `env.txt` records what the tool saw (O5); the band view's `END <k>` is the inventory's size (D53); under `fields = "json"` a stdout that is not ONE JSON object is observed as LINES — a divergence the truth names, never a discard (D53); an `[env]` key naming one of D6's six is REFUSED, as is a `cwd` or an emitted path that escapes the sandbox, `fields` other than `json`, `bands` without `fields`, and a `CLI_TIMEOUT` that is not a positive number (D52, D51).

**The core** — `lib/py/bbx/cli.py` grown from 90 to 579 lines, the one writer of every token now also the reader of the scenario and the runner of the tool: `load_scenario` (D46's seven keys and the `[env]` table; an unknown key or table REFUSED naming it), `resolve_tool` over `CLI_PATH` (`bbx.docset resolve`'s shape: per directory an executable `<set>` over `<set>.py`, the first directory that has either), `hermetic_env` (D6's set with the sandbox as `HOME` and `TMPDIR`, the `PATH` constant imported from `recount.py` so it is declared once), `run` (the out file and its band view removed first; `argv.txt`, `stdin.bin`, `env.txt` written into the sandbox before the tool runs; the tool under `subprocess.run` with the scrubbed dict, the scenario's stdin and `--timeout`; a negative return code is the guard; the declared emitted files read back or the run DISCARDED; the log through `observation()` and the band view through `band_view()`), `crash_log`, `summary_lines` (`NOTE: exit <n>` or `exit crash`, `NOTE: band-fields <n>`, `NOTE: emitted-files <n>`; a malformed log a FAIL line), `split_token` (by field name, the one splitter, BBX-12), and the SELF-TEST run before every tool run: FIPS 180-1's `"abc"` and the empty message as REFERENCE anchors for the hash the whole vocabulary rests on (§3.3: independent of us), the layout re-derived by `hashlib` directly (a second call path), the field layout with a band constant, the crash log's tail, the salt moving a token, a swapped pair of lines reading differently (BBX-15), the splitter on every kind and on five kinds of garbage, and the scenario reader on a synthetic scenario plus five refusals each named. The generator's truth logs regenerate byte for byte under the grown writer (`mkfakecli.py --check` ok, the same NOTE).

**The driver** — `drivers/cli.sh` (92 lines): the contract's face over the core; refuses the frame-driven family, the guard family, the document-set family and `CLI_KEEP_ENV` with exit 3 before anything runs; `CLI_TIMEOUT` (default 60, D51) validated; the sandbox a fresh `mktemp -d` removed after the run when omitted; `bbx.cli resolve`, the out file cleared, the self-test into `cli_selftest.txt`, `bbx.cli run` with `--nondet` under `CLI_NONDET`; exits 0 / 1 / 2 / 3 as D4 says, the tool's own non-zero exit an OBSERVATION. Its header carries the kind's two blind-spot sentences for the readout (RO2). `drivers/README.md` gains its row and the exit-2 line stops saying no BBX driver has a guard.

**The profile** — `log_summary = "python3 -m bbx.cli summary"` joins the `command-line` profile (D43 and D45 amended, as D45 said it would at step 2; `gates/config.sh` PASS on the changed dump).

**The gate** — `gates/cli_driver.sh` (portable, the 25th gate; 165 lines; `real 10.26` s): the core's self-test standalone; every scenario of the tree's fixture through the driver TWICE, byte-identical (BBX-14) and EQUAL to the generator's truth log (`cmp`) — nine of nine, `END n` equal to the point lines after the exit point; **two points re-hashed by `shasum`** (perl's Digest::SHA — not `bbx.sha1`, which is the same `hashlib` as the writer and would check nothing): `01_list` index 1 over the tool's first stdout line read directly, `03_show_json` index 2 over the canonical JSON value `"Maren"`; the band view's one row and `END 1` beside the constant token; the five summary NOTEs; O5's records read back against `08_join_stdin` (argv tail, the three stdin lines); `CLI_NONDET=1` moving the log; the two-component search path; an executable `<set>` that is an sh script; a scenario with no `[scenario]` table; six refusals with no log (`MASK_RANGES`, `POKES`, `GUARD_DEBUG`, `DOCSET_VIEW`, `CLI_KEEP_ENV`, `CLI_TIMEOUT=soon`) and the empty `MASK_RANGES` not one. Seven controls, each on a copy with the perturbation proven applied: `crash-vs-exit` (`--crash-at 3`: exit 2, `CRASH signal:6:SIGABRT` / `END-CRASH 3`, three lines kept and the third the truth's, beside `05_exit_1`'s `0 exit:1` green), `wrong-truth` (`06_unknown_option` green; its truth rewritten `0 exit:0` differs at line 1 and nowhere else), `unknown-scenario-key` (`argz` REFUSED, exit 3, no log), `sandbox-both-ways` (`--write-home` lands in the sandbox and never in the caller's HOME, absent before and after; with no sandbox argument the fresh dir under a private TMPDIR is gone after the run), `env-scrub` (the PREMISE measured first — the tool called directly under `FAKECLI_SALT=7` prints `salt=7` — then the same variable through the driver leaves the log the unsalted truth, `09_env`'s table reaches the tool with index 10 = `shasum('salt=9')` and `env.txt` exactly D6's six plus the table), `uncaptured-emitted-file` (`report` without `--emit`: exit 1 naming `report.json`, no log), `timeout` (`--sleep 5` under a wrapper driver's `CLI_TIMEOUT=1`: exit 1 naming the timeout, no log; `--sleep 1` under 10: exit 0, the truth). The gate SKIPs, asserting nothing, on a host with no `shasum`.

**Built in a shadow tree first** (`git archive HEAD`, `git init`, the gate through the classifier). The new gate was PASS on its first shadow run with 7 of 7 controls fired, and the five neighbouring gates (`config`, `controls`, `readout`, `tier`, `cli_fixture`) PASS after it; the shadow's whole portable tier under the runner was NOT GREEN on exactly one gate, `close_sweeps`: `defaults cited-not-registered D51 … D52` — ten citations in the new code, whose register rows had been written in the TREE's `docs/defaults.md` and were not in the shadow built from HEAD. The guard is BBX-24's mechanism firing on a sequencing fact, not a defect of the step; the tree, with the rows, read `close_sweeps` PASS (`defaults_rows=53 citations=461 unresolved=0`). No defect of the new code was found by the shadow or the tree this step (bbx-13's step found four); what stands between that and "it worked first time" is what the gate proves on purpose — seven perturbations each shown to have applied, the truth written by the design and not by a run, and two points re-hashed by an implementation with a different ancestry. Paid: one shadow tier run (~3 min) for the register finding. Learning (R27): a hazard line in `HANDOFF.md` — the shadow is built from HEAD and does not carry a register row written in the tree; write the row in the shadow too, or read its `close_sweeps` red as that.

## What it rests on

The gate run in the tree through the classifier with its exit tested (`cli_driver`, 7 controls, twice: once timed), `close_sweeps`, `rulings_shape` and `controls` the same way on the tree with the ledgers written; the five neighbouring gates and the whole portable tier in the shadow; the generator's `--check` on the tree under the grown writer; the two close batteries below.

## What this green does NOT assert

- **The comparators over these logs** (step 3): the truth is compared here with `cmp` and `diff`; no verdict line of the exact, set, schema or band family has met a command-line log, and `compare_set.py`'s row model is still the document-set's.
- **The suite over the fixture** (step 4): the identity refusal, the kinds loop's second consumer, RUN-FAIL on a crash log, `NOTE: band-fields` on the screen through `log_summary`, `--freeze`'s refusal to widen. The driver is exercised by its gate alone; `bin/bbx-run-suite` has not called it.
- **The adapters and the self subject** (step 5), **the file census as a gate** (step 6).
- **A real tool**: every input is the fixture's (class `fixture`) or a perturbed copy; D46's grammar, D52's environment and D53's shapes have one consumer, and the first real one is the detector (BBX-24, BBX-25). A tool that is a directory, a non-UTF-8 output, an emitted TREE, a tool that reads the terminal or the clock by contract (the plan's §9).
- **The interpreter**: `<set>.py` runs under the python3 that runs the harness; a consumer whose tool needs another interpreter ships an executable `<set>`.
- **That the crash log's shape suits anyone but this gate**: no comparator reads it by design (D53), and no second consumer has asked what it should carry.
- Any platform but Darwin arm64 (R21; the gate SKIPs without `shasum`). Executable controls (R29, S6): every `CONTROL FIRED` here is the gate's self-report.

```
  cli_driver: a tool that is a directory, a non-UTF-8 output, an emitted TREE, a fractional band (docs/plans/S4.md §9): a consumer's question, refused or discarded here, never measured
  band: the suite's loop over the band kind, `NOTE: band-fields` on the readout's screen and --freeze under the suite: S4 step 4
  band: that a rulings entry names a ruling that exists, or that the ruling names a mechanism: any non-empty id is accepted here (the thresholds gate's rule, R25; docs/plans/S4.md §9)
  band: anything about a real tool: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it; a fractional band or a band field that is not an integer by design is a consumer's question (D48)
  json_schema: the suite over the fixture — `NOT-EVALUATED (schema failed)` on the truth row of a scenario whose schema FAILed, and how the suite hands the JSON view to the schema family in place of the subject file: S4 step 4
  json_schema: the TSV format's verdicts beyond its vocabulary: gates/set_schema.sh freezes them; nothing here reads a TSV artifact
  json_schema: anything about a real tool: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it; a tool whose unordered output repeats a line cannot be frozen (a duplicate frozen row is hand-editing, BBX-17) — a consumer's question; the inside of a nested object is not judged (D49)
  census_recount: the truth of the not-recountable rows at their commits: they are named and counted, never run
  census_recount: that a hand-read citation says what its row claims: only that the line exists
  fidelity_bbh: anything about a suite, a comparator or an expectation: slice S2
  fidelity_bbh: bbh's example's correctness (G11 is bbh's to fix)
  fidelity_bbh: F15 unless BBX_FIDELITY_F15=1 was set for the run
  fidelity_bbh_s2: the kept suite run (--log) — bbh has none, so F12 diffs printed text only; the kept run's ground truth is gates/suite.sh
  fidelity_bbh_s2: anything about a real subject: F17's and F16b's inputs are synthesized, F16a's are the fake machine's (fixture class); the shapes are the lineage's paid-for cases and nothing else
  fidelity_bbh_s2: bbh's correctness: identical output on both sides is fidelity, not truth
  suite: any driver but the fake: a MAME or FBNeo driver is bbh's and untested here (bbh's own F8 rows)
  suite: the .sha1 kind's evidence: it is `self` class by construction (E4) and the register that says so is gates/provenance.sh's
  suite: the readout's reading of a kept suite run: gates/readout.sh reads the suite screen, gates/docset_suite.sh its coverage lines
  gates declaring no blind spot: 0   (a gate nobody has asked what its green leaves out)
```

**The open.** GREEN on `99016e3` (`build/selftest_20260910T185053Z`: `PASS 25    SKIP 0     FAIL 0     TIMEOUT 0  MISSING 0`; `controls fired 89 / declared 89`; `tree during the run: unchanged`; the two drift NOTEs the handoff predicted — bbh `447e5d2` one past the baseline, VampireSaved `6bead019` 25 past its census with 47 rows moved): the bbx-14 close re-derived. R21 the only open ruling; step 3 needs R36 and R40 (answered at bbx-12).

**The plan first (BBX-19), in its own commit `9567d7c`.** Two wordings of `docs/plans/S4.md` the built step differs from, each measured on the shadow build before the tree saw it. X20: §3's E1 `truth` row and C3 said `compare_exact.py` UNCHANGED for its second consumer (and §2's ancestors row "reused unchanged"); the first run of the family over a command-line log printed `FAIL exact: the truth END 9 does not equal its 10 indices` for every scenario — D47 puts the exit point at index 0 and makes `END <n>` the last index, and the family's END rule counted indices, which the document-set grammar (1..n) makes the same thing. One rule generalized — END counts the indices ABOVE ZERO — under which the 38 document-set lines `gates/set_schema.sh` freezes are byte-identical (PASS) and the nine command-line truths PASS. X21: §5's JSON row put the suite's `NOT-EVALUATED (schema failed)` line in `gates/json_schema.sh`, which runs no suite; it is step 4's gate's. One silence filled without a retraction: the schema is judged on the OBJECT, the kept run's log holds hashes only, so the driver writes the object's canonical text as a JSON view `<out>.json` beside the band view (D54). Gotcha G26 for the finding.

**The comparators.** `lib/py/bbx/compare_band.py` (new, 206 lines): the tolerant-numeric family over the driver's band view `<log>.bands` (D53's grammar re-read: `<i> <name>=<canonical value>`, `END <k>`, the indices 1..k, a duplicate name malformed); the spec D48's (`[spec]` class, baseset, `measured` required — a band with no measurement is a knob — and the optional `rulings` inline table; `[b<i>]` field, min, max integers, min ≤ max, a field frozen twice hand-editing, a rulings entry for a field no table freezes malformed); the INVENTORY compared both ways before any value (a frozen field the view lacks, a view field nobody froze — each FAILs naming it, BBX-13); then per field `FAIL band <field>: <v> outside [<min>, <max>]` naming all three, or `is not an integer`; `PASS band (<n> field(s), every value inside its band)` followed by `NOTE: band-fields <n>`. `--freeze <frozen> <proposed>` (D55): the proposed BANDS written in the generator's text (the class and the baseset the frozen file's, `measured` and `rulings` the proposed file's) when no band widens; a band that widens on either side needs its field in the proposed `rulings` or the freeze is REFUSED, exit 3, the file unchanged; a field in one file and not the other is REFUSED (the inventory never moves through a freeze); narrowing free. The `tolerant-numeric)` branch of `lib/sh/compare.sh` reaches it from the kind (R23) with the log alone — it reads the log's view and nothing else; `bin/bbx compare band`. `compare_schema.py`'s json format (D49; +142 lines, the tsv path's output unchanged): `[k<i>]` name and type in SORTED order (out of order malformed), R40's seven types (`bool` never an `int` or `number`), `items_op`/`items_n` required on a list and refused elsewhere; the artifact loaded as ONE JSON object or `FAIL schema: the artifact is not one JSON object`; the keys compared BOTH ways first (`frozen key '<k>' is absent from the object`, `key '<k>' is not frozen`), then one type each (`key '<k>' = <canonical value> is not <type>`), then the list count; `PASS schema (json: <n> keys)`; a type of the other format's vocabulary is `FAIL unknown schema type` under either format. `compare_set.py`'s line row shape (D56; +85 lines, the claim path's output unchanged): `[l<i>] line, sha1` told from the claim shape by the row's fields, a mixed file malformed, a frozen sha1 that is not its line's hand-editing; the run's rows the log's `line:` points through `bbx.cli.split_token`, a multiset compared by sha1 both ways, a frozen row named by its text and a run row by its sha1 (the log holds hashes); the shrink-only mode refused under this shape; the two trailing arguments accepted and not read. `compare_exact.py`: the END rule (8 lines, X20).

**The driver's JSON view (D54).** `bbx.cli.run` writes `<out>.json` — `canon(fields) + "\n"`, the same text the field hashes are over — whenever the observation was read as fields; removed with the log and the band view before every run (`drivers/cli.sh` clears the three). `01_list` writes none; `03_show_json` and `04_band` write one; `05_exit_1` (exit 1, no object) none. `mkfakecli.py --check` ok under the grown module, the NOTE unchanged.

**The gates.** `gates/band.sh` (portable, the 26th gate; 4.46 s measured once in the tree): the driver once on `04_band`, the view's value checked against the design's `size(4) = 265` RE-DERIVED from `BAND_BASE + (4 × BAND_MUL) % BAND_MOD`, never read off the view it judges; the log's index 2 the constant `field:size:band` (BBX-16); the pairing PASS through the dispatcher with the NOTE, `04_band`'s truth PASS beside it, `bin/bbx compare band`; twelve refusals each with its line (a class outside the family, the frame-driven profile, a spec that is not there, a log with no view, no `measured`, min above max, a fractional band refused by the subset parser, a rulings entry for a field no table freezes, a view value that is a float or a string, END disagreeing, no END, indices not 1..k). Four controls, each on a copy with the perturbation proven applied: `outside-the-band` (235 and 271 FAIL naming size, the value and `[236, 270]`; 236 and 270 PASS — inclusive both sides), `inventory-both-ways` (a view field nobody froze, a frozen field the view lacks, each named; both at once PASS with `NOTE: band-fields 2`), `ruling-less-class` (273 FAILs; a copy widened to 275 with no rulings entry REFUSED exit 3 unchanged by `cmp`; with `rulings = { size = "R36" }` written and 273 now PASSes; a narrower copy `[240, 270]` written with no ruling and 238 now FAILs; a copy adding a field REFUSED unchanged; the generator's own file re-frozen over itself byte-identical), `verdict-text` (a shadow `compare_band.py`). 21 distinct verdict lines frozen and classified; the freeze's 5 distinct lines frozen and not classified (a tool's answer, never a pairing's verdict — `finding.py` gains no word). `gates/json_schema.sh` (portable, the 27th; 7.06 s measured once): the driver on `01_list`, `02_unordered`, `03_show_json`; the JSON view beside the third and absent beside the first; every kind of the three PASS through the dispatcher — `PASS schema (json: 5 keys)`, `PASS set-inventory (9 rows, …)`, `PASS exact (10 indices, …)` with the exit point at index 0; eleven refusals (a format outside the two, an items op, a list shorter than frozen, keys out of sorted order, a list with no items claim, items on a non-list, the SUBJECT FILE handed as the artifact — what the suite passes today, step 4's open point — an absent artifact, a line-shaped set in shrink-only mode, a claim row in a file of line rows, a truth spec handed as the log). Five controls: `schema-before-values` (four shadow tools — `weight` renamed: schema names the frozen key absent and exact FAILs at index 2 where `mass` sorts; `weight + 1`: schema PASSes, exact FAILs at index 5; a key added: named as not frozen; `rank` a string: `key 'rank' = "6" is not int`), `two-vocabularies` (`hex` and `float` unknown under json; the document-set fixture's own TSV schema with `list` unknown under tsv), `unordered-both-ways` (a frozen row removed names the run's row by its sha1; added, by its text; duplicated, hand-editing; a wrong sha1, hand-editing; the run's last line dropped names Vesna as lost; a line repeated names the one surplus Quillon), `reversed-truth` (the ordered listing against its truth reversed FAILs exact at index 1; the same log PASSes the unordered inventory and FAILs the other scenario's truth at index 1 — the two listings differ in order alone and only the exact family sees it, D50), `verdict-text`. 30 distinct verdict lines frozen and classified.

**Built in a shadow tree first** (`git archive HEAD`, `git init`, the changes applied and committed there, the register rows too). The smoke run over the driver's logs — before any gate was written — found the END rule (above, G26; paid: one run of under a second, the plan corrected). `band.sh` PASS on its first shadow run, 4 of 4; `json_schema.sh` NOT GREEN on its first, one line: the gate's own key-swap `sed` (`id → name; name → id`) cancelled itself, and the comparator answered exactly what it was handed, `[k2] 'id' is not after 'id'` — a defect of the gate's perturbation, fixed through a placeholder (paid: one shadow gate run, 7 s); PASS on the second, 5 of 5. The eight neighbouring gates PASS through the classifier there (`set_schema` 8 s, `cli_driver` 12 s, `cli_fixture`, `compare_dispatch`, `controls`, `readout`, `close_sweeps` first time — the rows were in the shadow — `docset_suite` 58 s); then the shadow's whole portable tier under the runner: `PASS 23 SKIP 4 FAIL 0 MISSING 0`, `controls fired 85 / declared 85` (the four static gates SKIP without `BBX_BBH_HOME`; `band` 4 s, `json_schema` 7 s there). Then both new gates in the TREE through the classifier: PASS, 4 and 5 fired.

## What this green does NOT assert

And the loop's hand-over of the artifact to the schema family: today it passes the subject file, which the json format refuses (`FAIL schema: the artifact is not one JSON object`, frozen in `json_schema.sh`) — step 4's first decision, stated in `HANDOFF.md`.
- **That a `rulings` entry names a ruling that exists or a mechanism**: any non-empty id is accepted by the freeze (the thresholds gate's rule, R25); the register and the reviewer are where an id is checked.
- **The adapters and the self subject** (step 5), **the file census as a gate** (step 6).
- **A real tool**: every input is the fixture's (class `fixture`) or a perturbed copy or a shadow of its tool; a fractional band, a band field that is not an integer by design, a tool whose unordered output repeats a line, the inside of a nested JSON object — each a consumer's question, stated by the gates.
- **The TSV format beyond its vocabulary**: `gates/set_schema.sh` freezes it; `json_schema.sh` reads no TSV artifact but the two-vocabularies control's.
- Any platform but Darwin arm64 (R21). Executable controls (R29, S6): every `CONTROL FIRED` here is the gate's self-report.

```
what this green does NOT assert (declared by each gate's header):
  classify: that a verdict word printed by a gate outside the runner is read at all: the classifier reads exit status first, then the log; a PASS printed after a non-zero exit is FAIL by design
  config: the meaning of a consumer's keys: only that the layers resolve (consumer over kind profile over kind-blind default) and that dumps are stable
  tier: that a gate reaching an instrument through a path the source regex does not match is seen: the depth and the regex are the limit
  static_runner: the sweep runner or any gate that needs an instrument: this is the pre-commit chain only
  static_runner: runtimes as anything but this host under this load
  controls: that a control is RIGHT — only that a declared control fired and an undeclared one is red (docs/controls.md)
  sweep_runner: speed-up on a real consumer: the queue is measured on stub gates with sleeps, not on an instrument-tier suite
  sweep_runner: a gate that escapes its clone by an absolute path: clone-per-slot pins the cwd, nothing more
  sweep_runner: portability beyond macOS: mkfifo and exec 8<> are POSIX, not yet run on Linux or WSL
  fingerprint: the identity of any artifact that is not a single file: the kind-blind fingerprint is file-sha1 (D16)
  rulings_shape: the prose of a ruling: only its heading, its answer line and its DECISIONS row are read
  readout: that a declared blind spot is true or complete: the screen prints what the header says
  readout: the sweep runner's runs: only bbx-run-static --log and bbx-run-suite --log are read
  readout: that a register row's class is true of its file: the suite screen prints what the register says (gates/provenance.sh keeps it complete and inside the vocabulary)
  close_sweeps: that every corrected claim has a register row: the register is written by hand at the correction (a claim nobody registered is not swept)
  close_sweeps: step 9 of the close (the lineage untouched): that is the recount's clone and the fidelity gate's proof, not this gate
  temporal: anything about a real log: every shape here is synthesized (fixture class); the classes' fitness for a consumer is that consumer's ratification
  temporal: the dispatcher's spec line and the suite's dispatch (S2 steps 2 and 3): the comparators are called directly
  temporal: that the thresholds are right for any subject: they are the frame-driven profile's (D23) and a consumer's override needs R25's ruling row (gates/thresholds.sh)
  thresholds: that a ruling id in [thresholds].rulings names a ruling that exists: any non-empty string is accepted here; the consumer's rulings-shape gate is where an id is checked
  thresholds: the values' fitness for any subject: 2 / 60 / 8 are bbh's ratified policy carried in the frame-driven profile (D23), not a measurement of anything here
  compare_dispatch: the suite's dispatch around this library (which file is read, how many runs, the .sha1 and .diverge kinds' own paths): S2 step 3
  compare_dispatch: anything about a real log or a real mask: every log is synthesized and the masks are strings the guard compares, never applied
  expectation_kinds: the content of any expectation file: only its extension and its stem are read here
  expectation_kinds: the suite's use of the dispositions (S2 step 3)
  provenance: that a row's class is TRUE of its file: the register is written by hand at the freeze; only completeness and the vocabulary are checked
  provenance: bbh's example register (markdown, a consumer list of classes): it is bbh's and F19 (S6) reads it under R11
  docset_fixture: that any claim in the fixture is BOUND, MISMATCH or anything else (gates/docset_driver.sh and gates/set_schema.sh): only that the files are the generator's, the design is chiral, and the register is complete and tracked
  docset_fixture: the suite over the fixture: gates/docset_suite.sh
  docset_driver: the verdict text of the exact, set and schema comparators (S3 step 3): the truth is compared here with cmp and diff
  docset_driver: the suite over the fixture (identity, the kinds loop, the coverage NOTE on the screen): gates/docset_suite.sh
  docset_driver: prose, reasoning and causal claims in a document: only sentences in a declared form and the listed rows are claims
  docset_driver: the truth of the artifact itself: a document that agrees with a wrong artifact reads BOUND
  docset_driver: a nested document tree or a second artifact per set (DOCSET_VIEW is refused): S4 or a consumer's question
  set_schema: the suite's loop over the kinds, the kept-run rows (scenario, kind), NOT-EVALUATED on a schema FAIL and --freeze for the shrink-only kind: gates/docset_suite.sh
  set_schema: anything about a real document set: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it
  set_schema: the schema family on a second format or a second consumer (S4's JSON): tsv is its one format and the fixture's artifact its one consumer (BBX-25 unmet, stated)
  docset_suite: anything about a real document set: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it; the forms, guards and lexical classes are the fixture's (D35, D39, D40)
  docset_suite: bbh's precedence loop and its printed text: gates/fidelity_bbh_s2.sh (F12) and gates/suite.sh
  docset_suite: the readout screen beyond the lines checked here (the verdict, the pairings, the coverage and note lines, BBX-14, the driver's blind spots): gates/readout.sh
  docset_suite: a second consumer of the kinds loop (S4's command-line kind): one profile drives it here (BBX-25 unmet, stated)
  cli_fixture: the driver (S4 step 2): the tool is run here DIRECTLY by the generator's tool-check; no log in D47's grammar is produced by anything but the generator, so the truth logs describe the design, not a run
  cli_fixture: the comparators over the new kinds (`unordered`, `schema` json, `band`: S4 step 3) and the suite over the fixture (step 4): only that the files are the generator's, the design is chiral, the tool matches it, and the register is complete and tracked
  cli_driver: the verdict text of the exact, set, schema and band comparators over these logs (S4 step 3): the truth is compared here with cmp and diff
  cli_driver: the suite over the fixture (identity, the kinds loop, RUN-FAIL on a crash, the band NOTE on the screen): S4 step 4
  cli_driver: performance, behaviour on inputs outside the scenarios, and anything the tool wrote that the scenario did not declare
  cli_driver: a tool that is a directory, a non-UTF-8 output, an emitted TREE, a fractional band (docs/plans/S4.md §9): a consumer's question, refused or discarded here, never measured
  band: the suite's loop over the band kind, `NOTE: band-fields` on the readout's screen and --freeze under the suite: S4 step 4
  band: that a rulings entry names a ruling that exists, or that the ruling names a mechanism: any non-empty id is accepted here (the thresholds gate's rule, R25; docs/plans/S4.md §9)
  band: anything about a real tool: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it; a fractional band or a band field that is not an integer by design is a consumer's question (D48)
  json_schema: the suite over the fixture — `NOT-EVALUATED (schema failed)` on the truth row of a scenario whose schema FAILed, and how the suite hands the JSON view to the schema family in place of the subject file: S4 step 4
  json_schema: the TSV format's verdicts beyond its vocabulary: gates/set_schema.sh freezes them; nothing here reads a TSV artifact
  json_schema: anything about a real tool: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it; a tool whose unordered output repeats a line cannot be frozen (a duplicate frozen row is hand-editing, BBX-17) — a consumer's question; the inside of a nested object is not judged (D49)
  cli_suite: anything about a real tool: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it (D45, D46, D50)
  cli_suite: bbh's precedence loop and its printed text: gates/fidelity_bbh_s2.sh (F12) and gates/suite.sh; the document-set kind's loop: gates/docset_suite.sh
  cli_suite: the verdict text of the families beyond the lines frozen here: gates/band.sh, gates/json_schema.sh, gates/set_schema.sh
  cli_suite: the readout screen beyond the lines checked here (the verdict, the register's histogram, the classes, the notes, BBX-14, the driver's blind spots): gates/readout.sh
  cli_suite: which of the two writers of `band-fields` the screen should keep (R41, open): both are frozen here as printed
  census_recount: the truth of the not-recountable rows at their commits: they are named and counted, never run
  census_recount: that a hand-read citation says what its row claims: only that the line exists
  fidelity_bbh: anything about a suite, a comparator or an expectation: slice S2
  fidelity_bbh: bbh's example's correctness (G11 is bbh's to fix)
  fidelity_bbh: F15 unless BBX_FIDELITY_F15=1 was set for the run
  fidelity_bbh_s2: the kept suite run (--log) — bbh has none, so F12 diffs printed text only; the kept run's ground truth is gates/suite.sh
  fidelity_bbh_s2: anything about a real subject: F17's and F16b's inputs are synthesized, F16a's are the fake machine's (fixture class); the shapes are the lineage's paid-for cases and nothing else
  fidelity_bbh_s2: bbh's correctness: identical output on both sides is fidelity, not truth
  suite: any driver but the fake: a MAME or FBNeo driver is bbh's and untested here (bbh's own F8 rows)
  suite: the .sha1 kind's evidence: it is `self` class by construction (E4) and the register that says so is gates/provenance.sh's
  suite: the readout's reading of a kept suite run: gates/readout.sh reads the suite screen, gates/docset_suite.sh its coverage lines
  gates declaring no blind spot: 0   (a gate nobody has asked what its green leaves out)
```

**The open.** GREEN on `4a5cc61` (`build/selftest_20260910T195936Z`: `PASS 27    SKIP 0     FAIL 0     TIMEOUT 0  MISSING 0`; `controls fired 98 / declared 98`; `tree during the run: unchanged`; the two drift NOTEs the handoff predicted — bbh `447e5d2` one past the baseline, VampireSaved past its census): the bbx-15 close re-derived. R21 the only open ruling; step 4 needs R35 (answered at bbx-12).

**The decision the handoff left measured and open.** The kinds loop handed every family the SUBJECT FILE as its artifact (`bbx.fingerprint --path`), which for the json format is the wrong artifact — `gates/json_schema.sh` had measured it at bbx-15 (`FAIL schema: the artifact is not one JSON object`); the JSON view lives beside the log (D54), and the band family, for its part, derived `<log>.bands` on its own inside the dispatcher branch — a second resolver, implicit. Taken as the handoff recommended, under R23's answer ("the family and the VIEW are declared by the kind, never inside a spec line" — the maintainer's, 2026-09-10): **the kinds table gains a fourth column, the view** (D57: `-` none, `log` the run log itself, `subject` the subject file, `json` `<log>.json`, `bands` `<log>.bands`), in all four profiles' tables (`sha1`, `masked`, `diverge`, `truth`, `unordered` → `log`; the document-set `claims`, `covered`, `schema` → `subject`; the command-line `schema` → `json`, `band` → `bands`; `skip`, `pending` → `-`). `bin/bbx-run-suite` reads it off the table it already holds (`view_of`) and resolves it to a path in ONE function, `view_path`, checked for every EVAL kind at the entrance — after the identity, before any scenario runs (`FAIL: kind '<k>' declares view '<v>', which the kinds loop cannot resolve …`, exit 1, `finish RED`, no row) — and passes it per pairing as `compare_check`'s artifact argument to every family; the `tolerant-numeric)` branch hands `${8}` to `compare_band`, whose fourth argument is now the band view and which derives no path (one new frozen line: `FAIL band: the tolerant-numeric family needs the band view (compare_check's trailing argument)`). `expectations.py` reads four fields (a three-field row is refused on one `bbx expectations:` line, exit 1 — a consumer's table of the old shape fails the suite at the entrance, never reads as "no view"), prints four, and answers `view <ext>`. Not a new ruling: R23 ruled the principle; D25 and D45 are amended by D57's row and the amendment is in `DECISIONS_HISTORY.md` (BBX-24).

**The plan first (BBX-19), in its own commit `3d98d1d`.** Three wordings measured on the shadow build before the tree saw it. X22: §3 E1 and §8 step 4 said the loop changes by ZERO lines for its second consumer — 29 lines added and 5 removed (`git diff --numstat 4a5cc61 -- bin/bbx-run-suite`); BBX-25 is met for the loop all the same (nothing in it names a kind). X23: §3 C1/C2 had the band branch derive `<log>.bands` itself. X24: §6 had the screen read `exact, multiset, schema, tolerant-numeric` and `fixture 22, registry 1`; it reads `band, exact, multiset, schema` (the kept row's class column is the SPEC's class word) and `fixture 21` (12 files + 9 truth logs; the registry row is class `registry` and outside the set's histogram). D57 written in that commit, before its first use.

**R41 raised (open).** The first suite run over the fixture printed `NOTE: band-fields 1` TWICE under `04_band`: once from `compare_band.py` after its PASS (R36's letter: "the comparator … prints `NOTE: band-fields <n>`") and once from `bbx.cli summary` through `[suite].log_summary` (D43's command-line value, joined at step 2). `notes.tsv` keeps both rows; the screen prints `note: 04_band: band-fields 1` twice; under a band FAIL only the summary's survives. Both texts are the contributor's and no plan section showed them together, so the choice is queued for the maintainer with a recommendation (the summary alone: the observation side, survives a FAIL, counts a band nobody froze) and the screen is frozen AS PRINTED by the gate until it is answered — the gate checks the duplicate is visible, not hidden, and states it as NOT-ASSERTED.

**The gate.** `gates/cli_suite.sh` (portable, the 28th gate; 226 lines; 99.0 s measured once in the tree, 98.9 s in the shadow; 15 suite runs): the suite on the tree's fixture (read-only, checksummed before and after) — exit 0 and the 43 printed lines byte for byte (12 pairings, 28 NOTEs, the set line, `SUITE GREEN`), the 12 verdict lines each `pass` by `finding.py`, the kept rows keyed (scenario, kind) in the loop's order (schema first, then truth, unordered, band), the class column `band exact multiset schema`, `notes.tsv` 28 rows with `04_band band-fields 1` twice, `run.txt` loop=kinds expset=fixture pass=12 GREEN, the views the loop handed read back off the table (`truth=log unordered=log schema=json band=bands`); the second run byte-identical in results, notes and text, and the readout screen over both (the VERDICT line with scenarios 9 and pairings 12, `fixture 21`, the four class words, no coverage number stated as such, the band / exit / emitted-files notes, BBX-14 met, the driver's blind spots, the duplicate line counted); `--freeze` on a copy writing nothing (12 `authored` lines, no file changed, no `.sha1`). Seven controls, each on a copy with the perturbation proven applied: `identity-before-any-scenario` (one byte of the tool: UNREGISTERED, exit 1, no row; restored: GREEN), `nondeterministic-before-any-class` (a scenario copy with `--nondet`: that scenario NONDETERMINISTIC, kind `-`, RED, the other 11 pairings kept; a wrapper driver exporting `CLI_NONDET=1`: every scenario, 9 rows, no PASS; the caller's `CLI_NONDET=1`: scrubbed, GREEN; the driver directly under it: two differing logs), `crash-vs-refusal` (`--crash-at 3`: the driver's signal line then RUN-FAIL, kept run-fail with one row and no NOTE, the other scenarios pass; the key `argz`: the driver's REFUSED line naming it then RUN-FAIL the same way; `05_exit_1` and `06_unknown_option` PASS in the green run with `exit 1` / `exit 2` kept — three reports, BBX-4), `wrong-truth` (`0 exit:0` at index 0 of 06's truth: `FAIL exact: index 0 differs`, diverged, the other 11 pass), `schema-before-any-value` (`weight` → `width` in the frozen schema, the sorted order kept: `FAIL schema: frozen key 'width' is absent from the object` — the OBJECT, read from the JSON view the loop handed — the truth pairing `NOT-EVALUATED (schema failed)`, pending, fail=2, no NOTE for it, the other 8 scenarios pass), `band-through-the-loop` (the band narrowed to `[236, 260]`: `FAIL band size: 265 outside [236, 260]`, diverged, the truth pairing PASS on the constant token, `band-fields 1` kept ONCE — the summary's), `unknown-view` (a consumer copy whose table gives `schema` the view `objekt`: the entrance FAIL naming kind and view, exit 1, 0 rows, no NOTE, RED; a three-field table: one `bbx expectations:` line naming the row, the suite's FAIL line, exit 1, no kept run, no traceback).

**Also touched.** `gates/band.sh`: its 24 dispatcher calls hand the scenario and the band view (the loop's shape), one new frozen line (22 verdict lines frozen, was 21). The three readers of the kinds print — `gates/expectation_kinds.sh`'s frozen table string, `gates/cli_fixture.sh`'s and `gates/docset_fixture.sh`'s expected rows — read four fields. `compare.sh`: the header's contract line, the branch, `compare_band` (16 changed lines); the four S2/S3 dispatcher functions untouched (no diff line names `compare_exact`, `compare_set`, `compare_schema` or `compare_temporal`). `finding.py`, `fixture/`, the drivers, `readout.py`: 0 lines.

**Built in a shadow tree first** (`git archive HEAD`, `git init`, the changes applied and committed there, D57's row too). The first suite run over the fixture was GREEN in the shadow, the schema pairing PASSing over the JSON view — and printed the duplicate NOTE (R41). Then the neighbours through the classifier: seven fast gates PASS first time; `docset_suite.sh` NOT GREEN — the gate died on `cp` of a kept run the suite never wrote, and the suite run directly said `SUBJECT_FILE: unbound variable`: the entrance check read a variable resolved forty lines below it, and the command-line fixture, having no `subject` view, never reached that branch (G28; paid: one shadow gate run of 63 s and one direct run). The check moved after the identity; `docset_suite` PASS (6 fired). Then every perturbation measured on copies before the gate was written (13 suite runs in a scratch directory); the gate's first shadow run NOT GREEN on three defects of the gate itself — one NOTE value typed wrong, a verdict cut at a fixed column that `unordered` overruns, a header count of 16 where the run printed 15 (G29; paid: one gate run, 95 s); PASS on the second, 7 of 7. Then the patch applied to the tree (10 files, 337 insertions, 56 deletions) and the new gate in the TREE through the classifier: PASS, 7 fired, 99.0 s.

- Any platform but Darwin arm64 (R21). Executable controls (R29, S6): every `CONTROL FIRED` here is the gate's self-report.

```
what this green does NOT assert (declared by each gate's header):
  classify: that a verdict word printed by a gate outside the runner is read at all: the classifier reads exit status first, then the log; a PASS printed after a non-zero exit is FAIL by design
  config: the meaning of a consumer's keys: only that the layers resolve (consumer over kind profile over kind-blind default) and that dumps are stable
  tier: that a gate reaching an instrument through a path the source regex does not match is seen: the depth and the regex are the limit
  static_runner: the sweep runner or any gate that needs an instrument: this is the pre-commit chain only
  static_runner: runtimes as anything but this host under this load
  controls: that a control is RIGHT — only that a declared control fired and an undeclared one is red (docs/controls.md)
  sweep_runner: speed-up on a real consumer: the queue is measured on stub gates with sleeps, not on an instrument-tier suite
  sweep_runner: a gate that escapes its clone by an absolute path: clone-per-slot pins the cwd, nothing more
  sweep_runner: portability beyond macOS: mkfifo and exec 8<> are POSIX, not yet run on Linux or WSL
  fingerprint: the identity of any artifact that is not a single file: the kind-blind fingerprint is file-sha1 (D16)
  rulings_shape: the prose of a ruling: only its heading, its answer line and its DECISIONS row are read
  readout: that a declared blind spot is true or complete: the screen prints what the header says
  readout: the sweep runner's runs: only bbx-run-static --log and bbx-run-suite --log are read
  readout: that a register row's class is true of its file: the suite screen prints what the register says (gates/provenance.sh keeps it complete and inside the vocabulary)
  close_sweeps: that every corrected claim has a register row: the register is written by hand at the correction (a claim nobody registered is not swept)
  close_sweeps: step 9 of the close (the lineage untouched): that is the recount's clone and the fidelity gate's proof, not this gate
  temporal: anything about a real log: every shape here is synthesized (fixture class); the classes' fitness for a consumer is that consumer's ratification
  temporal: the dispatcher's spec line and the suite's dispatch (S2 steps 2 and 3): the comparators are called directly
  temporal: that the thresholds are right for any subject: they are the frame-driven profile's (D23) and a consumer's override needs R25's ruling row (gates/thresholds.sh)
  thresholds: that a ruling id in [thresholds].rulings names a ruling that exists: any non-empty string is accepted here; the consumer's rulings-shape gate is where an id is checked
  thresholds: the values' fitness for any subject: 2 / 60 / 8 are bbh's ratified policy carried in the frame-driven profile (D23), not a measurement of anything here
  compare_dispatch: the suite's dispatch around this library (which file is read, how many runs, the .sha1 and .diverge kinds' own paths): S2 step 3
  compare_dispatch: anything about a real log or a real mask: every log is synthesized and the masks are strings the guard compares, never applied
  expectation_kinds: the content of any expectation file: only its extension and its stem are read here
  expectation_kinds: the suite's use of the dispositions (S2 step 3)
  provenance: that a row's class is TRUE of its file: the register is written by hand at the freeze; only completeness and the vocabulary are checked
  provenance: bbh's example register (markdown, a consumer list of classes): it is bbh's and F19 (S6) reads it under R11
  docset_fixture: that any claim in the fixture is BOUND, MISMATCH or anything else (gates/docset_driver.sh and gates/set_schema.sh): only that the files are the generator's, the design is chiral, and the register is complete and tracked
  docset_fixture: the suite over the fixture: gates/docset_suite.sh
  docset_driver: the verdict text of the exact, set and schema comparators (S3 step 3): the truth is compared here with cmp and diff
  docset_driver: the suite over the fixture (identity, the kinds loop, the coverage NOTE on the screen): gates/docset_suite.sh
  docset_driver: prose, reasoning and causal claims in a document: only sentences in a declared form and the listed rows are claims
  docset_driver: the truth of the artifact itself: a document that agrees with a wrong artifact reads BOUND
  docset_driver: a nested document tree or a second artifact per set (DOCSET_VIEW is refused): S4 or a consumer's question
  set_schema: the suite's loop over the kinds, the kept-run rows (scenario, kind), NOT-EVALUATED on a schema FAIL and --freeze for the shrink-only kind: gates/docset_suite.sh
  set_schema: anything about a real document set: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it
  set_schema: the schema family on a second format or a second consumer (S4's JSON): tsv is its one format and the fixture's artifact its one consumer (BBX-25 unmet, stated)
  docset_suite: anything about a real document set: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it; the forms, guards and lexical classes are the fixture's (D35, D39, D40)
  docset_suite: bbh's precedence loop and its printed text: gates/fidelity_bbh_s2.sh (F12) and gates/suite.sh
  docset_suite: the readout screen beyond the lines checked here (the verdict, the pairings, the coverage and note lines, BBX-14, the driver's blind spots): gates/readout.sh
  docset_suite: a second consumer of the kinds loop (S4's command-line kind): one profile drives it here (BBX-25 unmet, stated)
  cli_fixture: the driver (S4 step 2): the tool is run here DIRECTLY by the generator's tool-check; no log in D47's grammar is produced by anything but the generator, so the truth logs describe the design, not a run
  cli_fixture: the comparators over the new kinds (`unordered`, `schema` json, `band`: S4 step 3) and the suite over the fixture (step 4): only that the files are the generator's, the design is chiral, the tool matches it, and the register is complete and tracked
  cli_driver: the verdict text of the exact, set, schema and band comparators over these logs (S4 step 3): the truth is compared here with cmp and diff
  cli_driver: the suite over the fixture (identity, the kinds loop, RUN-FAIL on a crash, the band NOTE on the screen): S4 step 4
  cli_driver: performance, behaviour on inputs outside the scenarios, and anything the tool wrote that the scenario did not declare
  cli_driver: a tool that is a directory, a non-UTF-8 output, an emitted TREE, a fractional band (docs/plans/S4.md §9): a consumer's question, refused or discarded here, never measured
  band: the suite's loop over the band kind, `NOTE: band-fields` on the readout's screen and --freeze under the suite: S4 step 4
  band: that a rulings entry names a ruling that exists, or that the ruling names a mechanism: any non-empty id is accepted here (the thresholds gate's rule, R25; docs/plans/S4.md §9)
  band: anything about a real tool: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it; a fractional band or a band field that is not an integer by design is a consumer's question (D48)
  json_schema: the suite over the fixture — `NOT-EVALUATED (schema failed)` on the truth row of a scenario whose schema FAILed, and how the suite hands the JSON view to the schema family in place of the subject file: S4 step 4
  json_schema: the TSV format's verdicts beyond its vocabulary: gates/set_schema.sh freezes them; nothing here reads a TSV artifact
  json_schema: anything about a real tool: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it; a tool whose unordered output repeats a line cannot be frozen (a duplicate frozen row is hand-editing, BBX-17) — a consumer's question; the inside of a nested object is not judged (D49)
  cli_suite: anything about a real tool: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it (D45, D46, D50)
  cli_suite: bbh's precedence loop and its printed text: gates/fidelity_bbh_s2.sh (F12) and gates/suite.sh; the document-set kind's loop: gates/docset_suite.sh
  cli_suite: the verdict text of the families beyond the lines frozen here: gates/band.sh, gates/json_schema.sh, gates/set_schema.sh
  cli_suite: the readout screen beyond the lines checked here (the verdict, the register's histogram, the classes, the notes, BBX-14, the driver's blind spots): gates/readout.sh
  cli_suite: that the two writers of `band-fields` AGREE: both counts trace to one `bands` list written once by
  census_recount: the truth of the not-recountable rows at their commits: they are named and counted, never run
  census_recount: that a hand-read citation says what its row claims: only that the line exists
  fidelity_bbh: anything about a suite, a comparator or an expectation: slice S2
  fidelity_bbh: bbh's example's correctness (G11 is bbh's to fix)
  fidelity_bbh: F15 unless BBX_FIDELITY_F15=1 was set for the run
  fidelity_bbh_s2: the kept suite run (--log) — bbh has none, so F12 diffs printed text only; the kept run's ground truth is gates/suite.sh
  fidelity_bbh_s2: anything about a real subject: F17's and F16b's inputs are synthesized, F16a's are the fake machine's (fixture class); the shapes are the lineage's paid-for cases and nothing else
  fidelity_bbh_s2: bbh's correctness: identical output on both sides is fidelity, not truth
  suite: any driver but the fake: a MAME or FBNeo driver is bbh's and untested here (bbh's own F8 rows)
  suite: the .sha1 kind's evidence: it is `self` class by construction (E4) and the register that says so is gates/provenance.sh's
  suite: the readout's reading of a kept suite run: gates/readout.sh reads the suite screen, gates/docset_suite.sh its coverage lines
  gates declaring no blind spot: 0   (a gate nobody has asked what its green leaves out)
```

| what | count | the command |

| disk held by them | 324 KB | `du -ch …/Python-*.ips …/Retired/Python-*.ips` |
| new reports from the tool's own `--crash-at 1` | 1 | count before, run, count after |
| new reports from the same process killed with SIGKILL | 0 | count before, run, count after |

Two of the reports on this host were made BY this sitting, deliberately, to
prove the mechanism — they are the positive control, not a symptom. The
counts above are measured once and are NOT gated: no check counts crash
reports across a battery today, which is the mechanism G30 proposes for S6.

## What it rests on

- The battery GREEN twice at one HEAD with the change staged, and the
  fidelity pairs unmoved on a plain clone of bbh at the baseline.
- R42's mechanism measured BOTH ways before it was recommended (BBX-5): the
  abort leaves exactly one host crash report, the SIGKILL death none; and the
  whole driver path measured on a COPY under TMPDIR, where the two crash logs
  differ by ONE line — `CRASH signal:6:SIGABRT` against
  `CRASH signal:9:SIGKILL` — with the same exit 2 and the same three lines
  kept before the death.
- The identity of the cause, not just its correlation: all 25 reports carry
  one signature, and the tool's `--crash-at` was run directly to produce a
  26th on demand.
- R41's ruling measured before it was answered, including the fact that
  decided it: the two `band-fields` counts cannot disagree under this driver.
- The 28 gates' own controls: 105 declared, 105 fired, none dead.

## What this green does NOT assert

- **That the harness leaves the host otherwise untouched.** One class of
  escaping artifact was found and fixed; nothing enumerates the others (a
  core file, a system log entry, a quarantine record, a launchd job). The
  driver's contract now NAMES the class; no check measures it.
- **That a future control will not reintroduce the reports.** The count is a
  habit written into `HANDOFF.md`, not a gate. A new control that needs a
  FAULT signal starts filing crash reports again and nothing here would say
  so — the maintainer, not a gate, was the detector this time (G30).
- **That the two writers of `band-fields` agree.** They cannot disagree under
  `drivers/cli.sh`, which writes both from one list; for a driver that wrote
  the log and the band view apart, nothing compares them (R41's residual).
- **That a real subject crashing under this driver leaves no host artifact.**
  It does. Only the FIXTURE's deliberate death was changed.
- **That a generated artifact's PROSE says anything true.** `mkfakecli.py
  --check` proves the tool equals the generator's output; both can be wrong
  together, and were this sitting (G31). No check reads a docstring.
- **Anything on a platform other than macOS.** R21's Linux or WSL run is
  still absent, so every green here is macOS-only.
- **That the lineage is where the census recorded it.** bbh and VampireSaved
  are both past their recorded HEADs; the drift is reported, not required
  equal (R18, R28).

# CLOSE — bbx-17 (2026-09-11)

**Close measurements.** Green first, twice, kept (`build/selftest_20260911T194343Z`, `build/selftest_20260911T200013Z`): `PASS 28    SKIP 0     FAIL 0     MISSING 0` / `PASS 28    SKIP 0     FAIL 0     MISSING 0`; `controls fired 105 / declared 105; gates with no declaration: 0; red: 0` in both; `ok: no tracked file changed during the run` in both; BBX-14 (more than one run): met — 28 gates, 0 verdict differences against the run started 2026-09-11T19:43:43Z at the same HEAD (the screen above). Both batteries ran on `9f16238` (the plan-first commit) with the change staged (porcelain 10 on the screen: the ten files the close commit carries before its ledgers were written).

**A third battery was run this sitting and does NOT count.** An earlier pair was run on a tool whose generated DOCSTRING was corrupted (G31); the first of that pair was GREEN — 28 gates, 105/105 — and is exactly why it is recorded here rather than quietly dropped: a green battery over a defective artifact is the sitting's sharpest measurement, not an embarrassment (§3.3 — `--check` proves the tool EQUALS its generator, and both were wrong together). Its kept directory was DELETED, the docstring repaired, and the two runs above started fresh. A fourth run was killed by the host for low memory partway through `fidelity_bbh_s2`, its partial directory deleted too; neither is quoted for anything.

**Sweeps:** `close_sweeps` and `rulings_shape` inside both batteries, and re-run once more on the FINAL tree after these ledgers were written, with `readout` beside them: `close_sweeps` PASS (retractions=25 retraction_hits=0 deferrals=0 defaults_rows=57 citations=676 unresolved=0, 3 controls), `rulings_shape` PASS (43 entries, 1 open, 42 answered, 42 decisions rows, 4 controls), `readout` PASS (7 controls). The close commit message quotes those verdicts.

**Lineage untouched, by construction and by proof:** `note: fidelity_bbh: bbh-drift baseline=10a82d2 tip=1af19c7 ahead=6`; `note: fidelity_bbh: bbh-source tip=1af19c7 porcelain=0 untouched-by-construction=clone` (clones only, clean after). bbh's tip did NOT move between the two batteries (`tip=1af19c727cb7 ahead=6` in both kept runs' `census_recount.log`), but it moved TWICE during the sitting — `777dbde` at the open battery, `1af19c7` by the close, from `02d58f3` at the bbx-16 close: reported, not required equal (R28's procedure when a sitting needs the current tip). VampireSaved `46ccb3b`, 36 ahead of its census, not re-measured by design. Fidelity F12–F17 unchanged.

**The host, measured because this sitting was about it.** `~/Library/Logs/DiagnosticReports/*.ips` held 4 Python crash reports at the close and held 4 after every run above: 2 from the batteries that ran BEFORE R42's change landed, and 2 this sitting created deliberately as the positive control. Zero were added by any gate after the change. This count is measured once and is NOT gated — the mechanism that would make it a measurement rather than a habit is written into G30 for S6.
