| commits that would move that identity | 22 of 53, and 5 of the last 10 | `git log --name-only -- bin lib drivers gates` (R44's cost) |
| the patch that carried the step from the shadow to the tree | 51 files, +2083 −36 | `git diff --cached --stat` in the shadow |
| defaults rows | 61 (D58–D61 added; D43 and D47 amended, dated) | `close_sweeps` prints `defaults_rows=61` |
| crash reports on the host, around the new gate's first tree run | 4 top + 92 Retired before, 4 + 92 after | `ls ~/Library/Logs/DiagnosticReports{,/Retired}/*.ips | wc -l` (G30, R42: zero growth) |
| `gates/suite.sh` at either bbh baseline | exit 0 and 77 identical printed lines both ways, `diff` empty | `gates/suite.sh` with and without `BBX_BBH_BASELINE=10a82d2` (G32, R43) |
| the orphan direction of BBX-9 | `bbx tier --unregistered` exits 0 in all three states; a dead row reds the run | the tool over a throwaway consumer, and an orphan planted in BBX's own `gates/` (G34, R45) |

## What it rests on

The two adapters were built in a shadow of the working tree and only then applied: the whole portable tier GREEN there (`PASS 25`, controls `98/98`, red 0) before the patch reached the tree, then `gates/adapters.sh` in the TREE through `bin/bbx classify` with its exit tested, then its controls report, then every neighbour that reads a gate HEADER or the changed core (`controls`, `readout`, `tier`, `static_runner`, `cli_driver`, `cli_suite`, `close_sweeps` — all PASS), then the battery with the step staged (29 gates, 111/111), then the two close batteries below. The refactor that gave three drivers one sandbox is proved by the two command-line gates being unchanged and green — and by two defects those gates and a diff-read caught first: a shared timeout message that had silently renamed the driver's subject, and an OSError path that would have named the interpreter instead of the tool. Both fixtures' expectations are `derived`, not `fixture`: the verdict words come from the frameworks' own stated contracts, and the generators write the truth logs from the design through `bbx.cli`'s vocabulary, never by running the adapter.

## What this green does NOT assert

- **The frameworks' own correctness.** Identical tokens mean the framework reported the same thing, never that what it reported is true — and these fixtures are written to report specific verdicts, so nothing here says a test suite or a gate battery is any good.
- **The DETAIL behind any verdict**: a traceback, a gate's printed lines, the `detail` and `seconds` columns of a kept run. The `seconds` column moved between runs of the same six stubs, which is why it is not mapped.
- **That an uncommitted edit to `bin`, `lib` or `drivers` moves the self subject's identity.** It does not: the key is of the COMMIT (R38). The kept run's `porcelain` line is what sees a dirty tree.
- **The ORPHAN direction of BBX-9, on any subject** (G34, R45 open): a gate on disk in no registry is NAMED and the run still exits 0, so the anti-orphan file in `fixture/selfgates` appears in no token and BBX's own battery would read green with an orphan in `gates/`.
- **Which bbh commit `gates/suite.sh` should clone** (G32, R43 open): it clones `f675710` where `D20` says `10a82d2`, and the 77 printed lines are identical at both — currency, not correctness.
- **Whether the refreeze cadence R38 implies is the right one** (R44 open): the identity moves on about every other commit, and this sitting paid it once.
- **The file census as a gate** (step 6): the census's "+kernel" rows still say what bbx-11 measured, although step 5 moved them.
- **A framework that is not on this host** (pytest, bats, node's runner): R37 declines them; a consumer adds a third adapter under the same core.
- Any platform but Darwin arm64 (R21). Executable controls (R29, S6): every `CONTROL FIRED` here is the gate's self-report.

# CLOSE — bbx-18 (2026-09-12)

**Close measurements.** Green first, twice, kept (`build/selftest_20260911T220942Z`, `build/selftest_20260911T222248Z`): `PASS 29    SKIP 0     FAIL 0     MISSING 0` / `PASS 29    SKIP 0     FAIL 0     MISSING 0`; `controls fired 111 / declared 111; gates with no declaration: 0; red: 0` in both; `ok: no tracked file changed during the run` and `tree during the run: unchanged` in both; BBX-14 (more than one run): met — 29 gates, 0 verdict differences at the same HEAD (the screen above). Both batteries ran on `4cbacd9` (the identity refreeze) with this close's ledgers staged (porcelain 5 on the screen: the five files this commit carries before the readout itself). A third battery ran earlier in the sitting, on `1afffc5` with step 5 staged, and was GREEN at the same 29 gates and 111 controls (`build/selftest_20260911T215227Z`) — its screen was lost to a backgrounded pipe through `tail` and read back off the kept run, which is now a hazard line. **Sweeps:** `close_sweeps` and `rulings_shape` inside all three batteries and re-run on the final tree after these ledgers were written (the commit message quotes both verdicts); each caught a real defect in this sitting's own documents first — a retracted wording left in HANDOFF's step-5 line, and an R-number inside the prose of DECISIONS.md's open-rulings line, which the gate read as a fourth open ruling. **Lineage untouched, by construction and by proof** (from the first close battery's `census_recount.log`, the recount's own clones): bbh `head=10a82d2 tree=clone tip=1af19c727cb7 ahead=6 porcelain=0` (79 rows, 78 match, 1 not recountable); VampireSaved `recorded=0cdd9726 tip=9f2891af00ea ahead=40 porcelain=436` — it moved twice DURING this sitting (37 ahead at the open, 40 now: another session works there, and its tree is nobody's baseline); SMS `head=ecc5481 tip=ecc5481d6f8d ahead=0 porcelain=0`. The two fidelity gates report `bbh-source tip=1af19c7 porcelain=0 untouched-by-construction=clone`. Fidelity F12–F17 unchanged; no verdict text moved on either side. **The battery's own cost moved:** ~10.5 min with `adapters` in it, past the ten-minute cap on a foreground command — the close ran each battery in the background and waited on its tally.

**Post-close (2026-09-12), one finding and no change to any verdict.** Asked what
was still running after the close was pushed, the answer was: four watcher loops,
sleeping on a condition that could never become false — `until ! pgrep -f
'bbx-run-static --config'` matches the watcher's OWN command line, so the wait
could not end (G37). No battery, gate or suite was running; both close batteries
had finished before the close commit and their verdicts were read off the kept
runs, so nothing above moves. What the finding adds is the sitting's THIRD
instance of one shape — a check that cannot reach its own failing state, beside
G34's registry report that cannot go red on an orphan and the plan's
clock-printing stub control that could not have fired (X32). The stray processes
were killed by PID, the project memory that had just recommended the defective
loop was corrected, and the hazard is in `HANDOFF.md`. Mechanism candidate for
S6, with R29: the runner already demands a gate's own FAIL from every declared
control; the same demand made of a WAIT would have caught this in one run.

# bbx-19 step 6 — the file census lifted into the tree and GATED; three defects in my own instrument, one of them visible only because a third kind exists (2026-09-12)

S4's build is complete. The census that measured BBX's genericity was itself
the last unmeasured thing in the tree: a scratchpad pair run by hand at bbx-11,
recorded verbatim in the census document's §C, read by no gate, and stale by one
step within a sitting. R39 ruled it into the tree and this step built it.

**What landed.** `lib/py/bbx/file_census.py` and `bbx file-census`; the portable
gate `gates/file_census_tool.sh` (13 s, 12 instrument runs on a synthetic
harness tree, 5 controls) which proves the INSTRUMENT every battery; the heavy
gate `gates/file_census.sh` (~20 min, 3 controls) which makes the MEASUREMENT
and is the first row of `gates/sweep.tsv` at the release scope; BBX's first
`[tier].patterns` entry, without which a gate registered only in the sweep
registry is an orphan; `expected/file_census.toml`, 44 frozen kind-sets, class
`self`, shrink-only; and `docs/census/bbx_files.md` regenerated, its §A and §B
never to be hand-edited again (BBX-21).

**Two things are now DERIVED where bbx-11 hand-listed them** (BBX-9: a
hand-maintained list is a smaller thing to forget to update). A kind's SEEDS
come from its own profile — the modules its comparator families reach, the
families from `[expectations].kinds` and the modules from `lib/sh/compare.sh`'s
own dispatch case followed through the functions it calls, plus its
`log_summary` module, its driver, and the driver of every tracked consumer
config of that kind. The F-by-need rule is a gate in the static registry whose
body, comments stripped, names `static_needs_env`. Measured: the second
reproduces bbx-11's hand list exactly (`fidelity_bbh` 4 mentions,
`fidelity_bbh_s2` 5, `suite` 10, `census_recount` 0), and the first ADAPTS
where a list would have lied — `compare_set.py` and `compare_schema.py`
correctly stopped discriminating the document set the moment the command-line
kind registered them.

**Three defects in this step's own instrument, each found by using it.**

The shadow is an instrumented harness, so R38's identity legitimately moves
inside it: `fixture/selfgates` printed "THE HARNESS HAS MOVED", the suite read
`UNREGISTERED build`, and `gates/adapters.sh` FAILed in the shadow — which the
contamination guard then correctly discarded as a ruined trace. Nothing was
broken; the census simply had no way to say "I changed this subject's content on
purpose". `--shadow-refreeze` runs one command inside the throwaway shadow,
after its first commit because the key is of the COMMIT, and the four identity
trees are untouched by the regeneration so the key is stable across the second
(G38).

With that fixed, the census filed `gates/adapters.sh` — a command-line gate —
as kind `DC`. `compare_exact.py` and `compare_set.py` both import `docset.py`,
the document-set seed, and the python half of the trace records what a process
LOADED, not what it ran. A seed hit now counts only when nothing else in the
same trace imports it, the importers derived transitively from the modules' own
import lines, and `adapters` reads `C`. **bbx-11 could not have seen this**:
with two kinds, every gate that ran the exact family WAS a document-set gate, so
the wrong attribution and the right answer were the same string. The third kind
is the detector of a defect in the tool whose job is measuring genericity, which
is BBX-25's own argument turned on itself (G39).

And keying a generated in-tree document by `HEAD` makes its own check fail for
ever, because the commit that writes the document moves the key — caught on the
portable gate's first run, as a one-field disagreement the gate could not
explain. The census is now keyed by R38's whole-set identity, which a docs-only
commit leaves alone and a commit touching the harness moves, which is exactly
when the census IS stale. Both directions are controls (G42, D62).

**The measurement, read off the run and not typed.** 30 gates traced in a
shadow of `9b16467`, every one PASS so no trace is contaminated; universe 44
files; seeds F=4 D=2 C=5; identity
`fe9928e94eac71039a4d6001c0c09a45a5618881`. Kinds: K 14, F 6, D 4, C 6.

The rows step 6 exists to move, against bbx-11's:

| file | bbx-11 | now |
|---|---|---|
| `bin/bbx` | 1 gate (`set_schema`), document-set only | 5 gates, document-set AND command-line |
| `bin/bbx-run-static` | 4 gates, frame-driven only (+kernel) | 5 gates, frame-driven AND command-line |
| `bin/bbx-run-sweep` | 2 gates, frame-driven only (+kernel) | unchanged, as X33 and X37 said it would be |

Eleven files are reached by gates of ALL THREE kinds, eleven more by the
document set and the command line, four by frames and the command line. **A17 =
0 files reached by NO gate**, printed explicitly now rather than left to be
inferred from a missing row — the one number this census exists to watch.

**Where the plan was wrong, corrected first in its own commits (BBX-19).** X36:
a gate in the sweep registry alone is an orphan, because `tier.py` reads
`known = portable | static` and exempts only an INSTRUMENT gate, so the step
added the pattern and retracted `bbx.toml`'s claim of an instrument-free gate
set. X37: the "+kernel" target named the wrong files — `bin/bbx` was never a
"+kernel" row and the sweep runner was never going to move. X38: the plan
expected six files reached by three kinds and the census measures three, because
no frame-driven gate CAN reach the exact comparators — that profile's one EVAL
family is `temporal` and its `exact` row is the kind-blind `sha1` with
disposition `N/A`, a hash comparison that calls no comparator.

**Two more defects, in my own working method rather than in the code.** A
housekeeping `git commit` with nothing to commit exits 1, and under `set -e`
that ended the portable gate four controls early with a log that simply stopped
(G40). And a `| tail -4` hid a traceback and handed me the pipe's exit instead
of the tool's, so a crashed regeneration read as a success — gotcha G16's own
family, on the sitting that added two gates against that class of mistake.

**One shape, now four instances in two sittings.** A check that cannot reach its
own failing state: the registry report that cannot go red on an orphan (G34), the
stub control that could not have fired (X32), the watcher loop that could not
exit (G37), and this sitting's retraction pattern that spanned two lines and so
could never match any line (G41). Three of the four read as good news at the
time. R45 and R29 are both about this, which is why they matter more than their
size suggests, and G41's entry names a third mechanism for S6: a sweep that
refuses a pattern it can never match, and reports a row never seen to fire as
unproven.

**Rulings raised, neither blocking.** R46 asks where the harness's own identity
key is computed, now that two writers of it exist in two languages and a gate
only cross-checks them on one tree; the precedent against merging is R41, which
KEPT two writers after measuring they could not disagree — these two can. R47
asks what detects a harness file no gate executes BETWEEN releases, now that the
census runs at release scope by its own ruling; the recommendation is a cheap
portable row holding the register complete both ways against the universe, which
cannot prove a file is reached and must say so.

## What it rests on

The instrument was built against a SYNTHETIC harness tree before it was ever
pointed at BBX, and that is the only reason its three logic defects were
findable: a tree whose traces are known by construction is the one place "a
file's category is the set of kinds of the gates that EXECUTED it" can be checked
both ways. Every number in §A and §B comes from a run whose 30 gates all PASSed
in the shadow — a gate that fails there has executed less than it executes in the
tree, so the run is REFUSED rather than adjusted, and that refusal is itself one
of the heavy gate's controls. Four runs now agree on every file count, kind and
verdict; the two that straddle the realpath fix agree exactly, which is what
established that the committed census's content was correct rather than merely
plausible. The frozen register is class `self` and says so: it is BBX's
measurement of BBX, which locks currency and never correctness (§3.3).

## What this green does NOT assert

- **That a file reached by a gate of some kind is USED by that kind.** The trace
  records what a process loaded or executed, never why. The explainer rule
  attributes an imported module to its importer, not to the importer's caller,
  and a module imported but never run is indistinguishable from one that ran.
- **A BBX-25 verdict.** The kind-specific rows are REPORTED. Whether a file
  reached by one kind only ought to be generic is a reading of this census, not
  its output.
- **That a harness file no gate executes would be caught BETWEEN releases**
  (R47 open). The census is the first row of `gates/sweep.tsv` at the release
  scope, and the portable gate proves the INSTRUMENT, not the tree. A module
  added and brought under no gate reads green until the next release run.
- **That the census's key has one writer** (R46 open). Two implementations
  compute R38's identity, in two languages, and a gate cross-checks them on BBX's
  own tree only — so they are proven equal about BBX and about nothing else.
- **Runtimes, anywhere.** They are out of the checked block on purpose and live
  in each kept run's `verdicts.tsv`.
- **What a CONSUMER's run executes.** Only what BBX's own registered gates
  execute, once each, on this host.
- **Anything about the orphan direction of BBX-9 on a registry** (G34, R45 open),
  **which bbh commit `gates/suite.sh` should clone** (G32, R43 open), or **the
  refreeze cadence** (R44 open) — all three unchanged by this step, and this
  sitting paid R44's cost a fifth time.
- Any platform but Darwin arm64 (R21). Executable controls (R29, S6): every
  `CONTROL FIRED` here is still the gate's self-report.

```
what this green does NOT assert (declared by each gate's header):
  classify: that a verdict word printed by a gate outside the runner is read at all: the classifier reads exit status first, then the log; a PASS printed after a non-zero exit is FAIL by design
  config: the meaning of a consumer's keys: only that the layers resolve (consumer over kind profile over kind-blind default) and that dumps are stable
  tier: that a gate reaching an instrument through a path the source regex does not match is seen: the depth and the regex are the limit
  static_runner: the sweep runner or any gate that needs an instrument: this is the pre-commit chain only
  static_runner: runtimes as anything but this host under this load
  controls: that a control is RIGHT — only that a declared control fired and an undeclared one is red (docs/controls.md)
  sweep_runner: speed-up on a real consumer: the queue is measured on stub gates with sleeps, not on an instrument-tier suite
  sweep_runner: a gate that escapes its clone by an absolute path: clone-per-slot pins the cwd, nothing more
  sweep_runner: portability beyond macOS: mkfifo and exec 8<> are POSIX, not yet run on Linux or WSL
  fingerprint: the identity of any artifact that is not a single file: the kind-blind fingerprint is file-sha1 (D16)
  rulings_shape: the prose of a ruling: only its heading, its answer line and its DECISIONS row are read
  readout: that a declared blind spot is true or complete: the screen prints what the header says
  readout: the sweep runner's runs: only bbx-run-static --log and bbx-run-suite --log are read
  readout: that a register row's class is true of its file: the suite screen prints what the register says (gates/provenance.sh keeps it complete and inside the vocabulary)
  close_sweeps: that every corrected claim has a register row: the register is written by hand at the correction (a claim nobody registered is not swept)
  close_sweeps: step 9 of the close (the lineage untouched): that is the recount's clone and the fidelity gate's proof, not this gate
  temporal: anything about a real log: every shape here is synthesized (fixture class); the classes' fitness for a consumer is that consumer's ratification
  temporal: the dispatcher's spec line and the suite's dispatch (S2 steps 2 and 3): the comparators are called directly
  temporal: that the thresholds are right for any subject: they are the frame-driven profile's (D23) and a consumer's override needs R25's ruling row (gates/thresholds.sh)
  thresholds: that a ruling id in [thresholds].rulings names a ruling that exists: any non-empty string is accepted here; the consumer's rulings-shape gate is where an id is checked
  thresholds: the values' fitness for any subject: 2 / 60 / 8 are bbh's ratified policy carried in the frame-driven profile (D23), not a measurement of anything here
  compare_dispatch: the suite's dispatch around this library (which file is read, how many runs, the .sha1 and .diverge kinds' own paths): S2 step 3
  compare_dispatch: anything about a real log or a real mask: every log is synthesized and the masks are strings the guard compares, never applied
  expectation_kinds: the content of any expectation file: only its extension and its stem are read here
  expectation_kinds: the suite's use of the dispositions (S2 step 3)
  provenance: that a row's class is TRUE of its file: the register is written by hand at the freeze; only completeness and the vocabulary are checked
  provenance: bbh's example register (markdown, a consumer list of classes): it is bbh's and F19 (S6) reads it under R11
  docset_fixture: that any claim in the fixture is BOUND, MISMATCH or anything else (gates/docset_driver.sh and gates/set_schema.sh): only that the files are the generator's, the design is chiral, and the register is complete and tracked
  docset_fixture: the suite over the fixture: gates/docset_suite.sh
  docset_driver: the verdict text of the exact, set and schema comparators (S3 step 3): the truth is compared here with cmp and diff
  docset_driver: the suite over the fixture (identity, the kinds loop, the coverage NOTE on the screen): gates/docset_suite.sh
  docset_driver: prose, reasoning and causal claims in a document: only sentences in a declared form and the listed rows are claims
  docset_driver: the truth of the artifact itself: a document that agrees with a wrong artifact reads BOUND
  docset_driver: a nested document tree or a second artifact per set (DOCSET_VIEW is refused): S4 or a consumer's question
  set_schema: the suite's loop over the kinds, the kept-run rows (scenario, kind), NOT-EVALUATED on a schema FAIL and --freeze for the shrink-only kind: gates/docset_suite.sh
  set_schema: anything about a real document set: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it
  set_schema: the schema family on a second format or a second consumer (S4's JSON): tsv is its one format and the fixture's artifact its one consumer (BBX-25 unmet, stated)
  docset_suite: anything about a real document set: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it; the forms, guards and lexical classes are the fixture's (D35, D39, D40)
  docset_suite: bbh's precedence loop and its printed text: gates/fidelity_bbh_s2.sh (F12) and gates/suite.sh
  docset_suite: the readout screen beyond the lines checked here (the verdict, the pairings, the coverage and note lines, BBX-14, the driver's blind spots): gates/readout.sh
  docset_suite: a second consumer of the kinds loop (S4's command-line kind): one profile drives it here (BBX-25 unmet, stated)
  cli_fixture: the driver (S4 step 2): the tool is run here DIRECTLY by the generator's tool-check; no log in D47's grammar is produced by anything but the generator, so the truth logs describe the design, not a run
  cli_fixture: the comparators over the new kinds (`unordered`, `schema` json, `band`: S4 step 3) and the suite over the fixture (step 4): only that the files are the generator's, the design is chiral, the tool matches it, and the register is complete and tracked
  cli_driver: the verdict text of the exact, set, schema and band comparators over these logs (S4 step 3): the truth is compared here with cmp and diff
  cli_driver: the suite over the fixture (identity, the kinds loop, RUN-FAIL on a crash, the band NOTE on the screen): S4 step 4
  cli_driver: performance, behaviour on inputs outside the scenarios, and anything the tool wrote that the scenario did not declare
  cli_driver: a tool that is a directory, a non-UTF-8 output, an emitted TREE, a fractional band (docs/plans/S4.md §9): a consumer's question, refused or discarded here, never measured
  band: the suite's loop over the band kind, `NOTE: band-fields` on the readout's screen and --freeze under the suite: S4 step 4
  band: that a rulings entry names a ruling that exists, or that the ruling names a mechanism: any non-empty id is accepted here (the thresholds gate's rule, R25; docs/plans/S4.md §9)
  band: anything about a real tool: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it; a fractional band or a band field that is not an integer by design is a consumer's question (D48)
  json_schema: the suite over the fixture — `NOT-EVALUATED (schema failed)` on the truth row of a scenario whose schema FAILed, and how the suite hands the JSON view to the schema family in place of the subject file: S4 step 4
  json_schema: the TSV format's verdicts beyond its vocabulary: gates/set_schema.sh freezes them; nothing here reads a TSV artifact
  json_schema: anything about a real tool: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it; a tool whose unordered output repeats a line cannot be frozen (a duplicate frozen row is hand-editing, BBX-17) — a consumer's question; the inside of a nested object is not judged (D49)
  cli_suite: anything about a real tool: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it (D45, D46, D50)
  cli_suite: bbh's precedence loop and its printed text: gates/fidelity_bbh_s2.sh (F12) and gates/suite.sh; the document-set kind's loop: gates/docset_suite.sh
  cli_suite: the verdict text of the families beyond the lines frozen here: gates/band.sh, gates/json_schema.sh, gates/set_schema.sh
  cli_suite: the readout screen beyond the lines checked here (the verdict, the register's histogram, the classes, the notes, BBX-14, the driver's blind spots): gates/readout.sh
  cli_suite: that the two writers of `band-fields` AGREE: both counts trace to one `bands` list written once by
  adapters: the frameworks' own correctness: identical tokens mean the framework reported the same thing, never that what it reported is true — and this gate's fixtures are written to report specific verdicts, so nothing here says a test suite or a gate battery is any good
  adapters: the DETAIL behind any verdict: a traceback, a gate's printed lines and the `detail` and `seconds` columns of a kept run are outside the observation (the seconds column moved between runs of the same six stubs at bbx-18, which is why it is not mapped)
  adapters: that an UNCOMMITTED edit to bin, lib or drivers moves the self subject's identity: it does not — the key is of the COMMIT (R38), and the kept run's `porcelain` line is what sees a dirty tree
  adapters: the ORPHAN direction of BBX-9 on the self subject: the anti-orphan file in the fixture is NAMED by the runner and appears in no token, because the runner exits 0 on an orphan (measured bbx-18, G34) — the verdict that direction needs is R45's
  adapters: the controls contract of the runner (`enforce`): the synthetic consumer sets it false, and gates/controls.sh is its ground truth
  adapters: the readout screen over these runs, and the comparators' own verdict text: gates/readout.sh, gates/set_schema.sh, gates/band.sh, gates/json_schema.sh
  adapters: a framework that is not on this host (pytest, bats, node's runner): R37 declines them, and a consumer adds a third adapter under the same core
  file_census_tool: that the census of BBX's OWN tree is correct: every trace here is the synthetic tree's (fixture class). gates/file_census.sh is the measurement; this gate is the instrument's ground truth
  file_census_tool: the SEED derivation on a real kinds table: this tree has no lib/sh/compare.sh and no kind-bearing consumer config, so F/D/C seeds are EMPTY here and only the static-need rule is exercised. The seeds are measured by gates/file_census.sh
  file_census_tool: that a gate PASSing in the shadow executed everything it executes in the tree: an uninstrumented path (a python module imported but never loaded, a file read and not sourced) is invisible by construction
  file_census_tool: any platform but this host's (R21)
  census_recount: the truth of the not-recountable rows at their commits: they are named and counted, never run
  census_recount: that a hand-read citation says what its row claims: only that the line exists
  fidelity_bbh: anything about a suite, a comparator or an expectation: slice S2
  fidelity_bbh: bbh's example's correctness (G11 is bbh's to fix)
  fidelity_bbh: F15 unless BBX_FIDELITY_F15=1 was set for the run
  fidelity_bbh_s2: the kept suite run (--log) — bbh has none, so F12 diffs printed text only; the kept run's ground truth is gates/suite.sh
  fidelity_bbh_s2: anything about a real subject: F17's and F16b's inputs are synthesized, F16a's are the fake machine's (fixture class); the shapes are the lineage's paid-for cases and nothing else
  fidelity_bbh_s2: bbh's correctness: identical output on both sides is fidelity, not truth
  suite: any driver but the fake: a MAME or FBNeo driver is bbh's and untested here (bbh's own F8 rows)
  suite: the .sha1 kind's evidence: it is `self` class by construction (E4) and the register that says so is gates/provenance.sh's
  suite: the readout's reading of a kept suite run: gates/readout.sh reads the suite screen, gates/docset_suite.sh its coverage lines
  gates declaring no blind spot: 0   (a gate nobody has asked what its green leaves out)
```

(porcelain 6 on the screen).

killed, and its partial kept run REMOVED rather than kept and explained.

**The census, separately from the battery.** `gates/file_census.sh` is not in any

three controls fired: `every tracked file under bin/, lib/ and drivers/ is executed
by at least one gate`, `kind-sets frozen 44 measured 44 failures 0 notes 0`, and
`docs/census/bbx_files.md is the run's own text`. That `--check` is the fourth run
to agree on every file count, kind and verdict.

**Sweeps.** `close_sweeps` and `rulings_shape` inside both batteries and re-run on
the final tree after every ledger was written: `close_sweeps=. files=142
retractions=38 retraction_hits=0 deferrals=0 defaults_rows=63 citations=779
unresolved=0 errors=0`; `rulings_shape` `open=6 answered=42 decisions_rows=42
errors=0`. Each caught a real defect in this sitting's own documents first: the
plan prose QUOTED the wording X36 retracts, twice; an R-number inside the prose of
DECISIONS.md's open-rulings line read as a seventh open ruling; and the gotcha
count exposed that G43 had been cited in a commit message and in both state pages
with no entry written.

# bbx-20 — four rulings answered, three built: one definition of the bbh baseline, the census register held every battery, and the deadlock that taught why (2026-09-12)

No step of a slice was built this sitting. The maintainer ruled on the waiting
queue and the work followed the rulings.

**R46 — agreed, in force, not yet built.** The harness's own identity key gets ONE
definition in the harness, with `fixture/selfgates/idkey.sh` becoming a shim. Its
build joins R44's, because both touch the single key that the fixture, the census
module and the census document all read.

**R43 — agreed and built.** `lib/sh/baseline.sh` is the one definition
(`bbx_baseline`), sourced by all three gates that read the baseline; the last of
them had cloned `f675710` for six sittings while its own header said it cloned the
baseline (G32). The config-key alternative the ruling offered was **declined by
measurement**: fidelity pair F13e diffs `bbh.config example/bbh.toml dump` against
BBX's, the two section lists are identical today, and a new kind-blind section
would appear in one and never the other. `gates/suite.sh` then printed exactly the
77 lines the ruling had predicted, and the override still reaches all three.

**R47 — agreed with one addition the maintainer chose when it was put to them, and
built.** `gates/census_register.sh` (portable, ~1 s, 3 controls) holds the census
register complete both ways on every battery: a tracked harness file with no
frozen row FAILS, a row naming a file the universe does not hold FAILS, and the
census being STALE is `NOTE: census-drift` — loud, never fatal, on R18's own split
between the register rotting and the harness moving. The accepted cost was stated
before the choice: a sitting that adds a harness file must run the census before
its close.

**R44 — agreed, for after this work.** Keep R38's key as ruled, `gates` included,
and make the refreeze a printed step. Measured for the next sitting: the mismatch
half is ALREADY red with both keys on the screen (seen twice at bbx-19), so only
the PASSING half may be missing, and that is to be measured before anything is
written.

**R45 — re-measured at the maintainer's request and rescoped from one direction to
three.** Step 6's own `[tier].patterns` entry had opened a second orphan class that
nothing reports (G45): an instrument-matching gate in no registry is not even
NAMED, where before the pattern existed every orphan was. Four directions now
exist across three registries, and one candidate fix was **measured and ruled out
rather than attempted** — the tier listing's registry column cannot say `sweep`,
because F13e diffs that listing and all five INSTRUMENT gates in bbh's example are
sweep-registered and print `-`.

**THE DEADLOCK, and why it is the sitting's most useful finding (G46).** R47's gate
asserts every harness file has a frozen census row. R43, the same sitting, added a
harness file. A row's kinds come only from a traced run, and the census refuses its
own run when a gate fails in the shadow — which the new gate did. Twenty minutes
discarded, and the only cure for the register was the thing the register blocked.
Reading the shadow's log made it sharper than staleness: the gate named TWO files
with no row, and the second was `lib/py/sitecustomize.py`, which the instrument
WRITES into the shadow and `git add -A` commits. The shadow's universe permanently
holds a harness file the tree's register can never contain, so the question is
**unanswerable there by construction**. Fixed without weakening the rule: the
instrument exports `BBX_FILE_CENSUS_SHADOW=1`, the gate SKIPs with that reason, and
the contamination rule splits — a FAIL discards a run, a SKIP does not, but a SKIP
under-reports what that gate reaches and so is NAMED both on the run and inside the
document's checked block.

**The census now proves R43 instead of my asserting it.** `lib/sh/baseline.sh` is
executed by exactly the three gates that source it, measured from what the
processes ran rather than from reading the code.

| the census: universe, gates traced, unreached | 45 / 31 / 0 | `bbx file-census --self` (§A rows A1, A2, A17) |
| gates that SKIPPED in the shadow | 1 (`census_register`) | named in the document's checked block and as a run NOTE |
| `lib/sh/baseline.sh`'s reach, measured by trace | 3 gates (`fidelity_bbh`, `fidelity_bbh_s2`, `suite`) | §B.2 — the independent proof that R43 landed |

## What this green does NOT assert

- **That `gates/file_census.sh` passes at THIS head.** It is release-scoped and was
  not re-run here; it was last green at bbx-19's head. What was verified instead, for
  seconds rather than twenty minutes, is that the document is the run's own text
  (`--reuse --check`) — and four earlier runs agree on every file count, kind and
  verdict. That is a check against the producing run, not a second independent run,
  and it is written here as such.
- **That a harness file is REACHED**, on any battery. `gates/census_register.sh`
  reads two file lists and one identity; only a traced run can answer reach, and it
  runs at the release scope (R47, D63).
- **The KINDS in any register row**, on any battery. A row claiming the wrong kinds
  passes the new gate and fails the release run.
- **The orphan direction of BBX-9 on the GATE registries** (G34, G45, R45 open) —
  now known to be unenforced in two directions, not one.
- **That the identity key has one writer** (R46 answered, not built): two
  implementations still compute it and a gate cross-checks them on BBX's own tree
  only.
- Any platform but Darwin arm64 (R21). Executable controls (R29, S6): every
  `CONTROL FIRED` is still the gate's self-report.

```
  cli_fixture: the comparators over the new kinds (`unordered`, `schema` json, `band`: S4 step 3) and the suite over the fixture (step 4): only that the files are the generator's, the design is chiral, the tool matches it, and the register is complete and tracked
  cli_driver: the verdict text of the exact, set, schema and band comparators over these logs (S4 step 3): the truth is compared here with cmp and diff
  cli_driver: the suite over the fixture (identity, the kinds loop, RUN-FAIL on a crash, the band NOTE on the screen): S4 step 4
  cli_driver: performance, behaviour on inputs outside the scenarios, and anything the tool wrote that the scenario did not declare
  cli_driver: a tool that is a directory, a non-UTF-8 output, an emitted TREE, a fractional band (docs/plans/S4.md §9): a consumer's question, refused or discarded here, never measured
  band: the suite's loop over the band kind, `NOTE: band-fields` on the readout's screen and --freeze under the suite: S4 step 4
  band: that a rulings entry names a ruling that exists, or that the ruling names a mechanism: any non-empty id is accepted here (the thresholds gate's rule, R25; docs/plans/S4.md §9)
  band: anything about a real tool: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it; a fractional band or a band field that is not an integer by design is a consumer's question (D48)
  json_schema: the suite over the fixture — `NOT-EVALUATED (schema failed)` on the truth row of a scenario whose schema FAILed, and how the suite hands the JSON view to the schema family in place of the subject file: S4 step 4
  json_schema: the TSV format's verdicts beyond its vocabulary: gates/set_schema.sh freezes them; nothing here reads a TSV artifact
  json_schema: anything about a real tool: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it; a tool whose unordered output repeats a line cannot be frozen (a duplicate frozen row is hand-editing, BBX-17) — a consumer's question; the inside of a nested object is not judged (D49)
  cli_suite: anything about a real tool: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it (D45, D46, D50)
  cli_suite: bbh's precedence loop and its printed text: gates/fidelity_bbh_s2.sh (F12) and gates/suite.sh; the document-set kind's loop: gates/docset_suite.sh
  cli_suite: the verdict text of the families beyond the lines frozen here: gates/band.sh, gates/json_schema.sh, gates/set_schema.sh
  cli_suite: the readout screen beyond the lines checked here (the verdict, the register's histogram, the classes, the notes, BBX-14, the driver's blind spots): gates/readout.sh
  cli_suite: that the two writers of `band-fields` AGREE: both counts trace to one `bands` list written once by
  adapters: the frameworks' own correctness: identical tokens mean the framework reported the same thing, never that what it reported is true — and this gate's fixtures are written to report specific verdicts, so nothing here says a test suite or a gate battery is any good
  adapters: the DETAIL behind any verdict: a traceback, a gate's printed lines and the `detail` and `seconds` columns of a kept run are outside the observation (the seconds column moved between runs of the same six stubs at bbx-18, which is why it is not mapped)
  adapters: that an UNCOMMITTED edit to bin, lib or drivers moves the self subject's identity: it does not — the key is of the COMMIT (R38), and the kept run's `porcelain` line is what sees a dirty tree
  adapters: the ORPHAN direction of BBX-9 on the self subject: the anti-orphan file in the fixture is NAMED by the runner and appears in no token, because the runner exits 0 on an orphan (measured bbx-18, G34) — the verdict that direction needs is R45's
  adapters: the controls contract of the runner (`enforce`): the synthetic consumer sets it false, and gates/controls.sh is its ground truth
  adapters: the readout screen over these runs, and the comparators' own verdict text: gates/readout.sh, gates/set_schema.sh, gates/band.sh, gates/json_schema.sh
  adapters: a framework that is not on this host (pytest, bats, node's runner): R37 declines them, and a consumer adds a third adapter under the same core
  file_census_tool: that the census of BBX's OWN tree is correct: every trace here is the synthetic tree's (fixture class). gates/file_census.sh is the measurement; this gate is the instrument's ground truth
  file_census_tool: the SEED derivation on a real kinds table: this tree has no lib/sh/compare.sh and no kind-bearing consumer config, so F/D/C seeds are EMPTY here and only the static-need rule is exercised. The seeds are measured by gates/file_census.sh
  file_census_tool: that a gate PASSing in the shadow executed everything it executes in the tree: an uninstrumented path (a python module imported but never loaded, a file read and not sourced) is invisible by construction
  file_census_tool: any platform but this host's (R21)
  census_register: that any file is REACHED by any gate. This gate reads two file lists and one identity; `gates/file_census.sh` is the measurement and runs at the release scope (D63, R47)
  census_register: the KINDS in the register. A row's `kinds` value is not read here at all — a row claiming the wrong kinds passes this gate and fails the release run (shrink-only, D62)
  census_register: the census DOCUMENT. Only the register is read; `docs/census/bbx_files.md` is checked by the release gate against a run's own text
  census_register: that the universe and the identity answer the same question. The universe is `git ls-files` and so includes a STAGED file, while the identity is of the COMMIT — a file staged and not committed is in one and not the other, which is the honest reading of both
  census_recount: the truth of the not-recountable rows at their commits: they are named and counted, never run
  census_recount: that a hand-read citation says what its row claims: only that the line exists
  fidelity_bbh: anything about a suite, a comparator or an expectation: slice S2
  fidelity_bbh: bbh's example's correctness (G11 is bbh's to fix)
  fidelity_bbh: F15 unless BBX_FIDELITY_F15=1 was set for the run
  fidelity_bbh_s2: the kept suite run (--log) — bbh has none, so F12 diffs printed text only; the kept run's ground truth is gates/suite.sh
  fidelity_bbh_s2: anything about a real subject: F17's and F16b's inputs are synthesized, F16a's are the fake machine's (fixture class); the shapes are the lineage's paid-for cases and nothing else
  fidelity_bbh_s2: bbh's correctness: identical output on both sides is fidelity, not truth
  suite: any driver but the fake: a MAME or FBNeo driver is bbh's and untested here (bbh's own F8 rows)
  suite: the .sha1 kind's evidence: it is `self` class by construction (E4) and the register that says so is gates/provenance.sh's
  suite: the readout's reading of a kept suite run: gates/readout.sh reads the suite screen, gates/docset_suite.sh its coverage lines
  gates declaring no blind spot: 0   (a gate nobody has asked what its green leaves out)
```

## Close measurements

**Green first, twice, kept, each run alone with nothing edited and a clean tree
before the pair** (`build/selftest_20260912T173058Z`, `build/selftest_20260912T174034Z`): `PASS 31  SKIP 0  FAIL 0  TIMEOUT 0  MISSING 0`
in both; `controls: fired 119 / declared 119; dead 0; undeclared firings 0; gates
red 0` in both; `each can fail: 31 of 31 gates proved a control fires on purpose`
in both; `tree during the run: unchanged` in both; BBX-14 met — `31 gates, 0
verdict differences against the run started 2026-09-12T17:30:58Z at the same
HEAD`. Both on `48a9525`, the reviewed refreeze, porcelain 0.

**Sweeps** on the final tree: `close_sweeps` exit 0 and `rulings_shape` exit 0
(`open=3 answered=45 decisions_rows=45 errors=0`). The rulings gate again caught a
real defect in this sitting's own ledgers first: naming a ruling id inside the
prose of the open-rulings line made it read as an extra open ruling.

**The refreeze came AFTER the census for the second sitting running**, which only
works because it touches `fixture/` alone and so moves neither the harness identity
nor the census key — verified both before and after: `af2b1f085070…` on both sides.



# bbx-21 — R21's first platform run: a CORRECT skip makes the battery NOT GREEN, and the defect is BBX's own contract (2026-09-13)

A short sitting with one subject. The maintainer produced the WSL run that R21 has
waited for since bbx-2, and it found something.

**The run.** `PASS 30  SKIP 1  FAIL 0  MISSING 0`, then `NOT GREEN`, with
`controls fired 115 / declared 119; red: 1` and the one red being
`controls=census_recount declared=4 fired=0 dead=4 verdict=RED`.

**The finding.** The skip was correct and predicted by the procedure: the three
lineage census files record absolute paths from the macOS host, so off this host
`gates/census_recount.sh` skips, asserting nothing. But a gate that skips runs none
of its checks, so none of its four declared must-fire controls can fire, and
`docs/controls.md` says "Declared and not fired is red". The reader called them
dead and the runner failed the battery. A correct skip is therefore unable to be
green on any host.

**Converted from a witness into an instrument the same day** (BBX-28), which is
what makes it a finding rather than a report: `BBX_CENSUS_DIR` pointed at a census
naming an absent tree reproduces the identical line on macOS. The defect is in
BBX's contract, not in WSL and not in any gate.

**Why this host could never have shown it.** Nothing skips here, because the three
lineage trees exist at the recorded paths, so every declared control always fires.
The second PLATFORM is the detector — BBX-25's argument about two consumers, applied
to hosts. Third time in three sittings that a second instance exposed what the first
could not: the third subject kind found the census's import attribution (G39), the
second consumer of the exact family found its END rule (G26), and now the second
host finds this.

**The larger half of the result: everything else is identical.** 31 registered
gates, the same per-gate declared counts summing to 119, the same anti-orphan and
working-tree results, both fidelity gates the same, and every NOTE-class number
equal across the two platforms — `verdict-lines-frozen` 22 / 30 / 38, `suite-runs`
15 / 17 / 25, `coverage 20/23`, `file-census-tool-runs 12`, `census-register` files
45 rows 45, and both fixtures' full count lines. Runtimes differ and are not gated;
the WSL host is faster on every heavy gate.

**Not fixed, deliberately.** The fix amends a contract R10 ruled, so R48 is raised
with its recommendation, four declined alternatives and a narrower fallback.
BBX-26 applies: the suite diverges on one platform until it is ruled.

## Counts, separately (measured this sitting by the commands named)

| what | count | how |
|---|---|---|
| the WSL run | `PASS 30  SKIP 1  FAIL 0  MISSING 0`, NOT GREEN | `docs/platforms/wsl/run_2026-09-13.txt`, a WITNESS |
| its controls | fired 115 / declared 119, red 1 | the same file |
| the reproduction on macOS | `declared=4 fired=0 dead=4 verdict=RED` | `BBX_CENSUS_DIR=<census naming an absent tree> gates/census_recount.sh`, then `bbx controls report` |
| gates and declared controls, both hosts | 31 and 119 | identical on the two screens |
| NOTE-class values that differ between hosts | 0 | the two screens compared line by line |
| gates whose runtime differs materially | 3 (`sweep_runner`, `cli_suite`, `adapters`) | WSL 58 / 59 / 37 s against 91 / 132 / 135 s — reported, never gated |
| gotchas, open rulings | G1–G47, 4 open | `docs/gotchas.md`; `rulings_shape` prints `open=4` |

## What this green does NOT assert

- **That the battery can be green on WSL.** It cannot, until R48 is ruled: the
  skip that is correct there is also what reds it. The platform ROW of R21 is
  therefore still *not green*, even though the run exists.
- **That the WSL run is a kept result.** It is the runner's printed output as
  reported, not a kept run directory, so it is a WITNESS under BBX-28. The finding
  it produced is instrument-grade; its counts are a filed report. The archive would
  upgrade it.
- **The census recount on any platform but this one.** It skipped, asserting
  nothing, by design.
- **A second architecture.** A second operating system only.
- Anything about `gates/file_census.sh` on WSL: release-scoped, not run there.

```
  tier: that a gate reaching an instrument through a path the source regex does not match is seen: the depth and the regex are the limit
  static_runner: the sweep runner or any gate that needs an instrument: this is the pre-commit chain only

  fidelity_bbh_s2: the kept suite run (--log) — bbh has none, so F12 diffs printed text only; the kept run's ground truth is gates/suite.sh
  fidelity_bbh_s2: anything about a real subject: F17's and F16b's inputs are synthesized, F16a's are the fake machine's (fixture class); the shapes are the lineage's paid-for cases and nothing else
  fidelity_bbh_s2: bbh's correctness: identical output on both sides is fidelity, not truth
  suite: any driver but the fake: a MAME or FBNeo driver is bbh's and untested here (bbh's own F8 rows)
  suite: the .sha1 kind's evidence: it is `self` class by construction (E4) and the register that says so is gates/provenance.sh's
  suite: the readout's reading of a kept suite run: gates/readout.sh reads the suite screen, gates/docset_suite.sh its coverage lines
  gates declaring no blind spot: 0   (a gate nobody has asked what its green leaves out)
```

# bbx-22 — R48 answered and built: a correct SKIP can be green, `--strict` still refuses it, and building it found G48 (2026-09-13)

One subject, in the order the maintainer set: R48 first, and R21 as far as this host
can take it, before S4 step 7.

**The ruling.** The maintainer agreed with R48's recommendation and added, in their
words: "--strict flag must not accept exceptions (attempting to do so MUST be a ruling
and I honestly doubt I'd ever rule in favor of it)". In force: a gate the runner's
classifier calls SKIP gets `verdict=SKIPPED`; its declared controls leave *fired /
declared* and are counted and named; only declared-and-not-fired is set aside; and
`--strict` makes every skip fatal, with no exception of any shape.

**What it changes on a screen.** On a host where `census_recount` skips — the WSL
run's one red — the runner now ends its controls block with `skipped: 1, whose 4
declared control(s) assert nothing` and reads GREEN; the readout's controls line ends
`skipped 1, whose 4 declared control(s) assert nothing: census_recount`, and the next
line says `skipped, proving nothing this run: census_recount`. On this host, where
nothing skips, the visible change is `; skipped 0` on the controls line and one more
declared blind spot, from `gates/controls.sh`.

**How it was proved, in the order that makes the proof mean something.**
1. The new checks were written first and run against the unfixed code: `gates/controls.sh`
   exit 1, the correct skip read `verdict=RED`; `gates/readout.sh` exit 1. The checks can
   see G47.
2. The fix landed; both gates exit 0.
3. Each of the three new R48 controls was shown DEAD on a regression planted in a scratch
   clone of the fix — a `--strict` exception (the run read GREEN), an exemption keyed off the
   SKIP marker instead of the verdict (an exit-2 gate read SKIPPED), and a skip forgiving a
   DEAD line (read SKIPPED, GREEN). None of them can only pass.
4. The real gate, `census_recount` alone with its census pointed at an absent tree: before
   the fix `dead=4 verdict=RED`, NOT GREEN, exit 1; after it `dead=0 verdict=SKIPPED`, GREEN,
   exit 0; after it under `--strict`, NOT GREEN, exit 1, for `--strict` alone.

**G48, found while building it.** The runner's controls verdict counted RED lines in the
reader's report and never asked whether the report existed. Measured in a scratch clone of
`fd63797` with a crash planted in the reader: `red: 0`, GREEN, exit 0, where the working
reader on the same consumer said NOT GREEN, exit 1. The readout of such a run said `each can
fail: 31 of 31` (measured on a copy of this sitting's opening run with the report emptied).
Fixed in the same block: one `controls=` line per gate that ran, or NOT GREEN naming the
shortfall; the readout names a gate with no line and counts proved gates from the lines it
has. Its control plants a crash in a shadow copy of the reader, and was DEAD before the fix
and FIRED after.

**R21 is not closed, and cannot be from here.** The run that closes its platform row has to
come from the WSL host again, at this close's commit or later. This host has no way to run
Linux: eleven candidate runtimes re-measured, all absent.

## What this green does NOT assert

- **That the REAL battery walks the R48 path on this host.** Nothing skips here, so every
  battery on this host takes the old path through the real gates. The new path is walked every
  battery by the stubs in `gates/controls.sh` and `gates/readout.sh`, and by the real gate once,
  by hand.
- **That WSL is green.** That needs the re-run.
- **That a SKIP is justified.** A skip for a bad reason sets its controls aside exactly like a
  good one; the screen names it and `--strict` refuses it, and nothing judges the reason (a new
  NOT-ASSERTED line in `gates/controls.sh`).
- **That no other runner in `bin/` counts a tool's output where it should require it.** G48's
  sweep has not been done.
- **That the census is fresh.** The drift NOTE carries until the regeneration planned after
  R44's and R46's build.

```
  file_census_tool: that a gate PASSing in the shadow executed everything it executes in the tree: an uninstrumented path (a python module imported but never loaded, a file read and not sourced) is invisible by construction
  file_census_tool: any platform but this host's (R21)

  census_register: the KINDS in the register. A row's `kinds` value is not read here at all — a row claiming the wrong kinds passes this gate and fails the release run (shrink-only, D62)

  census_register: that the universe and the identity answer the same question. The universe is `git ls-files` and so includes a STAGED file, while the identity is of the COMMIT — a file staged and not committed is in one and not the other, which is the honest reading of both
  census_recount: the truth of the not-recountable rows at their commits: they are named and counted, never run
  census_recount: that a hand-read citation says what its row claims: only that the line exists
```

## Close measurements

**Green first, twice, kept, each run alone with nothing edited while it ran**
(`build/selftest_20260913T123343Z`, `build/selftest_20260913T124821Z`): `PASS 31  SKIP 0  FAIL
0  TIMEOUT 0  MISSING 0` in both; `controls: fired 123 / declared 123; dead 0; undeclared
firings 0; gates red 0; skipped 0` in both; `each can fail: 31 of 31 gates proved a control fires
on purpose` in both; `tree during the run: unchanged` in both, porcelain 0 before and after the
pair; BBX-14 met — `31 gates, 0 verdict differences against the run started 2026-09-13T12:33:44Z
at the same HEAD`. Both on `429d3f8`. Declared controls moved 119 → 123: the four new controls of
`gates/controls.sh`. Gate runtimes summed 876 s and 837 s (not gated; the first run shared the
host with read-only work and a push).

**`429d3f8` was pushed BEFORE this close commit, and why (G49).** While the pair was running the
maintainer pulled for the WSL re-run and got `fd63797`, the remote's head, because the five bbx-22
commits were still local — the request had said "the close commit I'm about to push" before
anything was pushed. Their pasted run is therefore a second WSL run of the PRE-fix harness,
identical to the first (`PASS 30  SKIP 1`, `controls fired 115 / declared 119; red: 1`), and
evidence about nothing R48 changed. Its own lines identified the tree: `controls=controls
declared=2`, `census-drift … tree=1e40798f4530`, and no `skipped:` on the sum. `429d3f8` was pushed
once battery A read GREEN (the remote then read `429d3f84b3a4` by `git ls-remote`); this commit
adds only ledger text and the platform page, so the harness a WSL run meets is the same.

# bbx-23 — the combined build: G50 fixed, R45, R46 and R44 built, the census current again (2026-09-13)

One subject, chosen by the maintainer: build what was answered after the bbx-22 close, as one
step, so that the identity moves once, the census is regenerated once and the self subject is
refrozen once.

**What changed on the screen.**
- **G50.** A screen generated on a host that cannot read a gate's header now says `gates whose header
  was NOT FOUND: <n> under <dir> — their blind spots are UNKNOWN, not absent (G50)` instead of counting
  those gates as declaring no blind spot. A suite screen whose driver is missing says `header NOT FOUND`.
- **R45.** A new gate, `registry_complete`, is on every battery. It fails when a gate that needs no
  instrument is in neither the portable nor the static registry, when a gate that needs one has no
  sweep row, or when a sweep row names a gate that does not exist.
- **R44.** Every battery's notes now carry `adapters: self-identity whole-set=<key> program=<key> (R44:
  the key the registry row matched, printed on PASS)`.
- **R46.** Nothing visible: the identity key has one definition, and the old and new paths produce the
  same key.

**How it was proved.** A claim was corrected first (`f72a2e1`): R45's gate does not force a census
run, because the census universe is `bin/`, `lib/`, `drivers/` and not `gates/` — measured both ways
on a clone (G52). Then, in a scratch clone so the opening battery ran alone, each change's check was
written and run against the unchanged code, where it had to fail, and against the change, where it had
to pass. The patch went into the tree whole, identical to the clone file by file, and a full battery
ran over it uncommitted before the four commits. The census regeneration and the refreeze followed.

## Counts, separately (measured this sitting by the commands named)

| what | count | how |
|---|---|---|
| opening battery at `7b83477` | `PASS 31  SKIP 0  FAIL 0  TIMEOUT 0  MISSING 0`, GREEN; controls fired 123 / declared 123; tree unchanged | `bin/bbx selftest --log build/selftest_20260913T143456Z`, `bin/bbx readout` |
| a new gate file vs a new lib file, against the census register | gate file: `--check-register` exit 0, drift NOTE only; lib file: exit 1, `FAIL register` | a clone of `898dbe6`, one commit each — measured once, not gated (the register gate holds the lib direction) |
| the new checks on the UNCHANGED code | `readout.sh` exit 1 (`header-not-found-named` DEAD: `gates declaring no blind spot: 3`); `registry_complete.sh` exit 1 (3 controls DEAD: `unknown mode '--complete'`); `fingerprint.sh` exit 1 (the old command line) | a scratch clone of `7b83477` |
| the identity, three ways, on BBX's own tree | old `idkey.sh`, new shim and `file_census.identity`: wholeset `bc18c672fdb9…`, program `f21f241f49f8…`, equal | the clone, before and after R46 |
| the new checks on the CHANGED code | `readout.sh` exit 0, 8 fired, 30 ok; `registry_complete.sh` exit 0, 3 fired; `fingerprint.sh` exit 0, 2 fired; `tier.sh`, `controls.sh`, `static_runner.sh`, `file_census_tool.sh` (5 fired), `mkselfgates.py --check` all exit 0 | the clone |
| `bbx tier bbx.toml --complete` on BBX's own tree, before and after registering the new gate | exit 1, `FAIL orphan: registry_complete is instrument-free and in neither gates/portable.txt nor gates/static.txt`; then exit 0 | the clone |
| the pre-commit battery over the uncommitted build | `PASS 32  SKIP 0  FAIL 0`, GREEN; controls fired 128 / declared 128; each can fail 32 of 32; tree unchanged; gate runtimes sum to 860 s | `bin/bbx selftest --log build/selftest_20260913T145029Z` |
| the census regeneration | exit 0 in 848 s; 45 rows frozen, only `measured_at` changed; A2/A3 31 → 32, A8 15 → 16; then `--check-register` exit 0, files 45 rows 45, no drift | `bin/bbx file-census --self … --freeze`, `build/file_census_20260913T150603Z` |
| the refreeze | one line of `fixture/selfgates/expected/registry.tsv`, `bc18c672…` → `afd52caf…`; `mkselfgates.py --check` exit 0, printing `identity(wholeset)=afd52caf…` | `git diff --numstat -- fixture` |
| retraction positive controls | X43: 3 hits (HANDOFF ×2, STATE); X44: 7 hits (HANDOFF ×3, STATE ×3, the platform procedure) — each before its wording was rewritten | `gates/close_sweeps.sh` |

## What this green does NOT assert

- **That the WSL platform row attests this commit.** Its kept pair is at `429d3f8`; the readout, the tier
  classifier and the identity have changed since.
- **That an off-host screen's blind spots are known.** G50's fix names them `NOT FOUND`; it does not
  recover them.
- **That every registry entry is in the RIGHT registry, or that a dead portable/static row is caught by the
  new gate.** The runner already fails on the dead row; the right-tier question is not asked.
- **That the printed identity is checked as a value.** R44 prints it as a NOTE; the gate's check is that
  the registry row matches.
- **That no other absence in the harness reads as zero or silence.** G48's sweep, widened by G50, has not
  been done.

```
  docset_driver: prose, reasoning and causal claims in a document: only sentences in a declared form and the listed rows are claims

  cli_driver: the verdict text of the exact, set, schema and band comparators over these logs (S4 step 3): the truth is compared here with cmp and diff

  band: anything about a real tool: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it; a fractional band or a band field that is not an integer by design is a consumer's question (D48)

  json_schema: the TSV format's verdicts beyond its vocabulary: gates/set_schema.sh freezes them; nothing here reads a TSV artifact
  json_schema: anything about a real tool: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it; a tool whose unordered output repeats a line cannot be frozen (a duplicate frozen row is hand-editing, BBX-17) — a consumer's question; the inside of a nested object is not judged (D49)
  cli_suite: anything about a real tool: every input is the fixture's (fixture class) or a perturbed copy of it (D45, D46, D50)

  cli_suite: that the two writers of `band-fields` AGREE: both counts trace to one `bands` list written once by
  adapters: the frameworks' own correctness: identical tokens mean the framework reported the same thing, never that what it reported is true — and this gate's fixtures are written to report specific verdicts, so nothing here says a test suite or a gate battery is any good
  adapters: the DETAIL behind any verdict: a traceback, a gate's printed lines and the `detail` and `seconds` columns of a kept run are outside the observation (the seconds column moved between runs of the same six stubs at bbx-18, which is why it is not mapped)
  adapters: that an UNCOMMITTED edit to bin, lib or drivers moves the self subject's identity: it does not — the key is of the COMMIT (R38), and the kept run's `porcelain` line is what sees a dirty tree
  adapters: the ORPHAN direction of BBX-9 on the self subject: the anti-orphan file in the fixture is NAMED by the runner and appears in no token, because the runner exits 0 on an orphan (measured bbx-18, G34) — the verdict that direction needs is R45's
  adapters: the controls contract of the runner (`enforce`): the synthetic consumer sets it false, and gates/controls.sh is its ground truth
  adapters: the readout screen over these runs, and the comparators' own verdict text: gates/readout.sh, gates/set_schema.sh, gates/band.sh, gates/json_schema.sh
  adapters: a framework that is not on this host (pytest, bats, node's runner): R37 declines them, and a consumer adds a third adapter under the same core
  file_census_tool: that the census of BBX's OWN tree is correct: every trace here is the synthetic tree's (fixture class). gates/file_census.sh is the measurement; this gate is the instrument's ground truth
  file_census_tool: the SEED derivation on a real kinds table: this tree has no lib/sh/compare.sh and no kind-bearing consumer config, so F/D/C seeds are EMPTY here and only the static-need rule is exercised. The seeds are measured by gates/file_census.sh
  file_census_tool: that a gate PASSing in the shadow executed everything it executes in the tree: an uninstrumented path (a python module imported but never loaded, a file read and not sourced) is invisible by construction
  file_census_tool: any platform but this host's (R21)
  census_register: that any file is REACHED by any gate. This gate reads two file lists and one identity; `gates/file_census.sh` is the measurement and runs at the release scope (D63, R47)
  census_register: the KINDS in the register. A row's `kinds` value is not read here at all — a row claiming the wrong kinds passes this gate and fails the release run (shrink-only, D62)
  census_register: the census DOCUMENT. Only the register is read; `docs/census/bbx_files.md` is checked by the release gate against a run's own text
  census_register: that the universe and the identity answer the same question. The universe is `git ls-files` and so includes a STAGED file, while the identity is of the COMMIT — a file staged and not committed is in one and not the other, which is the honest reading of both
  registry_complete: that a gate is in the RIGHT registry: an instrument-free gate registered static that could run portable passes here
  registry_complete: a dead portable or static row: bin/bbx-run-static already reads it as MISSING and fails, and the fidelity pairs depend on that text
  registry_complete: an instrument reached through a path the tier's source regex does not match — that is gates/tier.sh's blind spot, inherited here
  census_recount: the truth of the not-recountable rows at their commits: they are named and counted, never run
  census_recount: that a hand-read citation says what its row claims: only that the line exists
  fidelity_bbh: anything about a suite, a comparator or an expectation: slice S2
  fidelity_bbh: bbh's example's correctness (G11 is bbh's to fix)
  fidelity_bbh: F15 unless BBX_FIDELITY_F15=1 was set for the run
  fidelity_bbh_s2: the kept suite run (--log) — bbh has none, so F12 diffs printed text only; the kept run's ground truth is gates/suite.sh
  fidelity_bbh_s2: anything about a real subject: F17's and F16b's inputs are synthesized, F16a's are the fake machine's (fixture class); the shapes are the lineage's paid-for cases and nothing else
  fidelity_bbh_s2: bbh's correctness: identical output on both sides is fidelity, not truth
  suite: any driver but the fake: a MAME or FBNeo driver is bbh's and untested here (bbh's own F8 rows)
  suite: the .sha1 kind's evidence: it is `self` class by construction (E4) and the register that says so is gates/provenance.sh's
  suite: the readout's reading of a kept suite run: gates/readout.sh reads the suite screen, gates/docset_suite.sh its coverage lines
  gates declaring no blind spot: 0   (a gate nobody has asked what its green leaves out)
```

**Lineage untouched, by construction and by proof** (from battery A's `census_recount.log`, the

# bbx-24 — R49: a header entry is one line, and that is checked; the census current again; the stale spot fixed (2026-09-13)

One subject, found by reading the opening screen rather than by any gate, and ruled by the maintainer the
same sitting (R49): two blind spots the screen printed cut mid-sentence (G53). Then the stale counts
HANDOFF and the platform README carried (G54), and S4 step 7.

**What changed on the screen.**
- **G53 / R49.** A blind spot whose header entry runs past its one line is printed with `  <gate>: ^
  TRUNCATED — the blind spot above runs on <n> more header line(s) that this screen does not print (one
  line per entry: docs/controls.md, G53)` directly under it — on the battery screen for a gate, on a suite
  screen for a driver. No such line appears on this sitting's screens: the two entries that ran on are
  one line each since `398a64f`, and `cli_suite`'s blind spot now reads to its end.
- **The close sweep** prints `header_entries=<n> continued=<n>` and fails the battery on any `*.sh` header
  entry that runs on, naming the file, the line and the kind.
- `close_sweeps` declares one more blind spot: a blind spot or a control written as prose, without its
  key, is read by nobody.

**How it was proved.** The layout was measured before the rule was written. The headers were corrected
first, in their own commit, their texts proven identical once joined. Each new check was run against the
old code in a scratch clone, where it had to read DEAD, and against the new, where it had to fire; the
new sweep failed the unfixed tree by name and passed the fixed one. A battery ran over the uncommitted
build before the commits; the census regeneration and the refreeze followed.

## What this green does NOT assert

- **That every keyed header line is one line.** R49's rule covers `MUST-FIRE:` and `NOT-ASSERTED:` entries in
  `*.sh` headers; a `SKIP:`, `READ-ONLY (…)` or `PORTABILITY:` line that runs on is not checked, and no
  screen prints those texts today.
- **That a blind spot is written as one.** A sentence that should have been a `NOT-ASSERTED:` entry and was
  written as prose is read by nobody, and nothing notices.
- **That the earlier screens are corrected.** The 7 committed screens carrying the cut line stay as they were
  generated (BBX-21); G53 names them.
- **That the WSL platform row attests this commit.** Its kept pair is at `429d3f8`.
- **That no other reader stops short.** G48's sweep, widened by G50 and G53, has not been done.

## What the slice's green does NOT assert

- Anything about a REAL command-line tool: every `fakecli` expectation is fixture-class (its screen:
  `PASSed on a real pairing: none`); D51's 60 s and the fixture's design counts are the fixture's, their
  detector the first real consumer.
- That a real test suite or gate battery is any good: the unittest and selfgates pairings rest on `derived`
  expectations over code written here, and both adapter drivers declare that identical tokens mean the
  framework reported the same thing, never that what it reported is true.
- A framework that is not on this host (pytest, bats, node's runner: R37), a non-UTF-8 output, an emitted
  tree, a fractional band, the inside of a nested JSON object (`docs/plans/S4.md` §9, D49).
- Executable controls: every one of the 131 `CONTROL FIRED` lines is a gate's self-report (R29, S6).
- That the file census sees everything a gate touches: an uninstrumented path — a module imported and never
  loaded, a file read and not sourced — is invisible by construction, and `census_register` SKIPs in the
  shadow, so what it reaches is under-reported there (both named in the census).
- The release-scoped measurement's own controls at this commit: `gates/file_census.sh` was not run this
  sitting; its register and document were regenerated by the tool it drives.
- Any platform but Darwin arm64 at this commit: the WSL pair attests `429d3f8` (R21).
- bbh's tip `529f9d2`, 8 past the baseline (R28 when needed).
- That every keyed header line is one line (R49 checks `MUST-FIRE:` and `NOT-ASSERTED:` only), or that a
  blind spot is written as one.
- The registers' TRUTH: that a row's class is true of its file, and that a default's class is right —
  completeness and vocabulary are checked; the classes are their authors'.

tool or a real test suite. The maintainer rules S4 closed or not on this table.
